## M07 — Pass 1 Grok: throughput families and order-budget assembly

````````text
$ python3 research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/S14a_2_record_measurements.py --section M07
$ cat research/pde_ledger_v3/directives/_measurements/S14a_2_review_witnesses/p1-grok_steady_throughput_and_budget.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Objects for the S14a part 2 decision-list review.

Prints residuals and values. No verdicts.
"""

import sympy as sp

r, w, t = sp.symbols("r w t", real=True)
h0, s, n0, C0 = sp.symbols("h0 s n0 C0", positive=True)
b, gamma0 = sp.symbols("b gamma0", real=True)
omega, B = sp.symbols("omega B", real=True)


def material_residual(n, ur, uw):
    return sp.simplify(
        sp.diff(n, t)
        + (1 / r**2) * sp.diff(r**2 * n * ur, r)
        + sp.diff(n * uw, w)
    )


def order_residual(n, ur, uw, chi, gamma_b):
    return sp.simplify(
        sp.diff(chi * n, t)
        + (1 / r**2) * sp.diff(r**2 * (chi * n * ur), r)
        + sp.diff(chi * n * uw, w)
        - n * gamma_b
    )


def projected_storage(n, chi, h):
    sigma = sp.integrate(n, (w, -h / 2, h / 2))
    sigma_chi = sp.integrate(chi * n, (w, -h / 2, h / 2))
    return sp.simplify(sigma), sp.simplify(sp.diff(sigma, t)), sp.simplify(sigma_chi), sp.simplify(
        sp.diff(sigma_chi, t)
    )


h = h0 + s * t
xi = w / h
n = n0
uw = (s / h) * w
ur = -(s / h) * r / 3 + C0 / r**2

print("SECTION 1 homologous spreading flow")
print("material_residual", material_residual(n, ur, uw))

# Family S: rates are functions of the spreading coordinate only.
chi_s = sp.Integer(1) / 2
gamma_return_s = xi**2
gamma_drain_s = xi**2
gamma_b_s = sp.simplify(gamma_return_s - gamma_drain_s)
print("family_S_gamma_B", gamma_b_s)
print("family_S_order_residual", order_residual(n, ur, uw, chi_s, gamma_b_s))
dt_n_gamma_return_s = sp.simplify(sp.diff(n * gamma_return_s, t))
print("family_S_dt_n_Gamma_return", dt_n_gamma_return_s)
print(
    "family_S_dt_n_Gamma_return_at_w_h0_t0",
    sp.simplify(dt_n_gamma_return_s.subs({w: h0, t: 0})),
)
sigma_s, dt_sigma_s, sigma_chi_s, dt_sigma_chi_s = projected_storage(n, chi_s, h)
print("family_S_projected_material", sigma_s)
print("family_S_dt_projected_material", dt_sigma_s)
print("family_S_dt_projected_order", dt_sigma_chi_s)
print("family_S_face_speed_minus_interior_uw_lower", sp.simplify(uw.subs(w, -h / 2) - sp.diff(-h / 2, t)))
print("family_S_ur", ur)
print("family_S_uw", uw)

# Family E: Eulerian-constant source densities, secular order, same spreading flow.
chi_e = sp.Integer(1) / 2 + b * t
gamma_return_e = b + gamma0
gamma_drain_e = gamma0
gamma_b_e = sp.simplify(gamma_return_e - gamma_drain_e)
print("SECTION 2 Eulerian-constant source densities")
print("family_E_order_residual", order_residual(n, ur, uw, chi_e, gamma_b_e))
print("family_E_dt_n_Gamma_return", sp.simplify(sp.diff(n * gamma_return_e, t)))
print("family_E_dt_n_Gamma_drain", sp.simplify(sp.diff(n * gamma_drain_e, t)))
print("family_E_dt_chi", sp.simplify(sp.diff(chi_e, t)))
_, dt_sigma_e, _, dt_sigma_chi_e = projected_storage(n, chi_e, h)
print("family_E_dt_projected_material", dt_sigma_e)
print("family_E_dt_projected_order", dt_sigma_chi_e)
# Range on a finite interval, concrete numbers.
chi_e_num = sp.Lambda(t, chi_e.subs(b, sp.Rational(1, 10)))
print("family_E_chi_at_0", chi_e_num(0))
print("family_E_chi_at_1", chi_e_num(1))

# Family A: faces fixed, Eulerian-stationary, flow only the core piece.
h_a = h0
ur_a = C0 / r**2
uw_a = 0
chi_a = sp.Integer(1) / 2
gamma_return_a = gamma0
gamma_drain_a = gamma0
print("family_A_material_residual", material_residual(n, ur_a, uw_a))
print(
    "family_A_order_residual",
    order_residual(n, ur_a, uw_a, chi_a, sp.simplify(gamma_return_a - gamma_drain_a)),
)
print("family_A_dt_n_Gamma_return", sp.diff(n * gamma_return_a, t))
sigma_a, dt_sigma_a, _, dt_sigma_chi_a = projected_storage(n, chi_a, h_a)
print("family_A_dt_projected_material", dt_sigma_a)
print("family_A_dt_projected_order", dt_sigma_chi_a)
print("family_A_minus_family_S_dt_projected_material", sp.simplify(dt_sigma_a - dt_sigma_s))

# Product versus rate.
n_var, gamma_var = sp.symbols("n_var gamma_var", cls=sp.Function)
print("SECTION 3 product versus rate")
n1 = 2 + t
g1 = 3
g2 = 1 / (2 + t)
print("dt_Gamma_when_product_changes", sp.diff(g1, t))
print("dt_n_Gamma_when_rate_constant", sp.diff(n1 * g1, t))
print("dt_n_Gamma_when_product_constant", sp.simplify(sp.diff(n1 * g2, t)))
print("dt_Gamma_when_product_constant", sp.simplify(sp.diff(g2, t)))

# omega -> 0 of a modulation on the spreading profile.
print("SECTION 4 omega to 0 on the spreading profile")
gamma_family = xi**2 * (1 + B * sp.sin(omega * t))
gamma_limit = sp.limit(gamma_family, omega, 0)
print("omega_limit_of_Gamma_return", sp.simplify(gamma_limit))
print("dt_of_that_limit", sp.simplify(sp.diff(n * gamma_limit, t)))
print(
    "dt_of_that_limit_at_w_h0_t0",
    sp.simplify(sp.diff(n * gamma_limit, t).subs({w: h0, t: 0})),
)
# A storage term that a frequency limit drops, and the piece it keeps.
sigma_mode = sp.symbols("Sigma0")
sigma_rate, amplitude = sp.symbols("sigma_rate A", real=True)
storage_mode = sp.diff(sigma_mode + sigma_rate * t + amplitude * sp.sin(omega * t), t)
print("storage_before_limit", sp.simplify(storage_mode))
print("storage_omega_limit", sp.simplify(sp.limit(storage_mode, omega, 0)))
print("storage_dropped_term", sp.simplify(storage_mode - sp.limit(storage_mode, omega, 0)))

# Material balance has no source densities. Expression difference under the constraint is empty.
print("SECTION 5 material balance contains neither rate")
n_sym, ur_sym, uw_sym = sp.symbols("n u_r u_w", cls=sp.Function)
material_expr = sp.diff(n_sym(r, w, t), t) + (1 / r**2) * sp.diff(
    r**2 * n_sym(r, w, t) * ur_sym(r, w, t), r
) + sp.diff(n_sym(r, w, t) * uw_sym(r, w, t), w)
print("Gamma_return_in_material", material_expr.has(sp.Symbol("Gamma_return")))
print("material_free_symbols", sorted(str(s) for s in material_expr.free_symbols))
print("material_expression_minus_itself", sp.simplify(material_expr - material_expr))

# Order-budget assemblies.
print("SECTION 6 order budget assemblies")
fna_m, b2_m, b1, fna_p, b2_p, b3 = sp.symbols(
    "fna_minus b2_minus b1_minus fna_plus b2_plus b3_plus"
)
int_n_return, int_n_drain = sp.symbols("int_n_Gamma_return int_n_Gamma_drain")
lower_before = -(fna_m + b2_m)
upper_before = fna_p + b2_p
lower_after = -b1
upper_after = b3
c_minus = fna_m + b2_m - b1
c_plus = -fna_p + b3 - b2_p
int_n_gamma = int_n_return - int_n_drain
# Interior integration of the order equation, per unit area:
# dt + div + before_sum - int_n_gamma = 0.
# Re-derived full-source form uses after_sum and face coefficients.
before_sum = lower_before + upper_before
after_sum = lower_after + upper_after
full_source = int_n_gamma + c_minus + c_plus
# If the interior identity holds, this residual is the full balance's failure
# when crossing is taken to be the before terms and the source includes faces.
residual_before_plus_full_source = sp.simplify(before_sum - after_sum + (full_source - int_n_gamma) - (c_minus + c_plus) + (after_sum - before_sum - (c_minus + c_plus)))
# Directly: full identity is dt+div+after_sum - full_source = dt+div+before_sum - int_n_gamma
#   + (after_sum - before_sum - c_minus - c_plus)
gap_after_versus_before = sp.simplify(after_sum - before_sum - c_minus - c_plus)
print("C_minus", sp.simplify(c_minus))
print("C_plus", sp.simplify(c_plus))
print("after_minus_before_minus_C", gap_after_versus_before)
print("before_minus_after", sp.simplify(before_sum - after_sum))
# Concrete nonzero face jump.
concrete = {fna_m: 5, b2_m: 7, b1: 3, fna_p: 11, b2_p: 13, b3: 17}
print("concrete_C_minus", c_minus.subs(concrete))
print("concrete_C_plus", c_plus.subs(concrete))
print("concrete_before_minus_after", (before_sum - after_sum).subs(concrete))
print("concrete_gap_after_before_C", gap_after_versus_before.subs(concrete))

# Sphere orientation.
a, R = sp.symbols("a R", positive=True)
H = sp.Function("H_r")
divergence_integral = sp.integrate(sp.diff(r**2 * H(r), r), (r, a, R))
outward_from_origin = lambda radius: radius**2 * H(radius)
split_that_matches = sp.simplify(
    outward_from_origin(R) - outward_from_origin(a) - divergence_integral
)
split_both_plus = sp.simplify(
    outward_from_origin(R) + outward_from_origin(a) - divergence_integral
)
print("orientation_residual_outer_minus_inner", split_that_matches)
print("orientation_residual_both_plus", split_both_plus)
print(
    "orientation_residual_both_plus_value",
    split_both_plus.subs({H(a): 4, H(R): 9, a: 2, R: 5}),
)

# Totals against part 1's order-lost sign.
order_lost = -(int_n_gamma + c_minus + c_plus)
total_plain_difference = int_n_return - int_n_drain
total_order_lost_sign = -int_n_return - (-int_n_drain)
print("order_lost_minus_drain_plus_return_plus_faces", sp.simplify(order_lost - (-int_n_return + int_n_drain - c_minus - c_plus)))
print(
    "plain_totals_minus_order_lost_without_faces",
    sp.simplify(total_plain_difference + order_lost),
)
print(
    "plain_totals_relation_residual_without_faces_concrete",
    sp.simplify(total_plain_difference + order_lost).subs(
        {int_n_return: 8, int_n_drain: 3, **concrete}
    ),
)
print("order_lost_sign_totals_difference_from_plain", sp.simplify(total_order_lost_sign - total_plain_difference))

# Dropping J_chi from the in-plane order flux.
print("SECTION 7 order flux and far radius")
Jr, Jw = sp.symbols("J_r J_w", cls=sp.Function)
chi_f = sp.Function("chi")
n_f = sp.Function("n")
ur_f = sp.Function("u_r")
uw_f = sp.Function("u_w")
full_radial = chi_f(r, w, t) * n_f(r, w, t) * ur_f(r, w, t) + Jr(r, w, t)
advective_radial = chi_f(r, w, t) * n_f(r, w, t) * ur_f(r, w, t)
print(
    "radial_order_flux_minus_advective",
    sp.simplify(full_radial - advective_radial),
)
# Shell face channel depends on the outer radius when the face density is live.
face_density = sp.Function("face_density")
area = 4 * sp.pi * r**2
face_channel = sp.integrate(area * face_density(r, t), (r, a, R))
print("d_face_channel_d_outer", sp.simplify(sp.diff(face_channel, R)))
print(
    "d_face_channel_d_outer_at_sample",
    sp.simplify(sp.diff(face_channel, R).subs({face_density(R, t): 1, R: 5, sp.pi: sp.pi})),
)
# Lower limit of the shell integral is the inner radius, not the origin.
shell_integral = sp.Integral(area * face_density(r, t), (r, a, R))
print("shell_integral", shell_integral)
print("shell_limits", shell_integral.limits)
````````

## M08 — Pass 2 Codex: long-time average versus a slow family

````````text
$ python3 research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/S14a_2_record_measurements.py --section M08
$ cat research/pde_ledger_v3/directives/_measurements/S14a_2_review_witnesses/p2-codex_check_dc_vs_slow.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Two exact solution probes, not an S14a construction or acceptance test."""
import resource
resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_AS, (2 * 1024**3, 2 * 1024**3))
import sympy as s

t, r, w = s.symbols('t r w', real=True)
T, om = s.symbols('T omega', positive=True)
c = s.Rational(1, 2)

def out(name, x):
    print(name + ' = ' + str(s.simplify(x)))

def div(ur, uw):
    return s.diff(r**2 * ur, r) / r**2 + s.diff(uw, w)

print('A: t>=0, omega>0, 1<=r<=2, faces 0 and h=1+t/4; n=1 in all regions')
print('A: chi=1/2 inside, zero outside; J_chi=0; same velocity on both sides of each face')
h = 1 + t/4
n = s.S.One
ur = (-2+s.cos(om*t))/(r**2*h)
uw = s.S.Zero
gr = gd = 1/(r**2*h)
out('A u_r', ur)
out('A Gamma_return=Gamma_drain in the interior', gr)
out('A material regular residual', s.diff(n,t)+div(n*ur,n*uw))
out('A order regular residual', s.diff(c*n,t)+div(c*n*ur,c*n*uw)-n*(gr-gd))
aminus = uw
aplus = uw - s.diff(h,t)
Cminus = c*n*aminus
Cplus = -c*n*aplus
out('A material face jump residuals', s.Tuple(n*aminus-n*aminus,n*aplus-n*aplus))
out('A order face coefficients', s.Tuple(Cminus,Cplus))
sigma = s.integrate(n,(w,0,h))
F = s.integrate(n*ur,(w,0,h))
K = s.diff(s.integrate(r**2*sigma,(r,1,2)),t)
face = s.integrate(r**2*(-n*aminus+n*aplus),(r,1,2))
core = -(r**2*F).subs(r,1)
phi = (r**2*F).subs(r,2)
J = face+core
src = s.integrate(r**2*(s.integrate(n*(gr-gd),(w,0,h))+Cminus+Cplus),(r,1,2))
ret = s.integrate(r**2*s.integrate(n*gr,(w,0,h)),(r,1,2))
drain = s.integrate(r**2*s.integrate(n*gd,(w,0,h)),(r,1,2))
out('A return,drain totals/(4*pi)', s.Tuple(ret,drain))
out('A storage,face,core,J,outer flux/(4*pi)', s.Tuple(K,face,core,J,phi))
out('A material shell residual/(4*pi)', K+J+phi)
out('A order shell residual/(4*pi)', c*K+c*core+c*phi-src)
meanJ = s.integrate(J,(t,0,T))/T
dcJ = s.limit(meanJ,T,s.oo)
slowJ = s.limit(J,om,0,dir='+')
out('A finite-window mean J/(4*pi)', meanJ)
out('A DC J/(4*pi), omega>0', dcJ)
out('A fixed-t omega->0 J/(4*pi)', slowJ)
out('A DC minus slow J/(4*pi)', dcJ-slowJ)
out('A discarded J/(4*pi) in omega->0', s.limit(J-dcJ,om,0,dir='+'))
out('A DC projected material storage', s.diff(sigma,t))
out('A mean projected density', s.integrate(sigma,(t,0,T))/T)
out('A mean projected density T->infinity', s.limit(s.integrate(sigma,(t,0,T))/T,T,s.oo))

print('B: fixed faces 0,1; n=1+cos(omega*t)/4; chi=1/2 inside, zero outside; J_chi=0')
n = 1+s.cos(om*t)/4
ndot = s.diff(n,t)
ur = (-1/r**2-r*ndot/3)/n
gr = gd = 1/(r**2*n)
zz = s.symbols('zz',real=True)
out('B density range', s.calculus.util.function_range(1+zz/4,zz,s.Interval(-1,1)))
out('B u_r', ur)
out('B material regular residual', s.diff(n,t)+div(n*ur,0))
out('B order regular residual', s.diff(c*n,t)+div(c*n*ur,0)-n*(gr-gd))
fixed_face_factor = uw-s.diff(s.S.Zero,t)
out('B material and order face coefficients', s.Tuple(n*fixed_face_factor-n*fixed_face_factor,n*fixed_face_factor-n*fixed_face_factor,c*n*fixed_face_factor,-c*n*fixed_face_factor))
out('B return,drain totals/(4*pi)', s.Tuple(s.integrate(r**2*n*gr,(r,1,2)),s.integrate(r**2*n*gd,(r,1,2))))
volume = r**2*ur
out('B volume flux/(4*pi)', volume)
out('B log(n) derivative minus n_dot/n', s.diff(s.log(n),t)-ndot/n)
out('B log(n) change over one period', (s.log(n).subs(t,2*s.pi/om)-s.log(n).subs(t,0)))
z = s.symbols('z',real=True)
# Tangent half-angle substitution in the period integral of 1/n.
inverse_mean = s.integrate(2/(s.Rational(5,4)+s.Rational(3,4)*z**2),(z,-s.oo,s.oo))/(2*s.pi)
out('B period mean 1/n', inverse_mean)
out('B DC volume flux/(4*pi), omega>0', -inverse_mean)
out('B fixed-t omega->0 volume flux/(4*pi)', s.limit(volume,om,0,dir='+'))
out('B DC minus slow volume flux/(4*pi)', -inverse_mean-s.limit(volume,om,0,dir='+'))
out('B period mean n', s.integrate(n,(t,0,2*s.pi/om))/(2*s.pi/om))
out('B period mean outer number flux/(4*pi)', s.integrate((r**2*n*ur).subs(r,2),(t,0,2*s.pi/om))/(2*s.pi/om))
````````

## M09 — Pass 2 Codex: spreading and the order of limits

````````text
$ python3 research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/S14a_2_record_measurements.py --section M09
$ cat research/pde_ledger_v3/directives/_measurements/S14a_2_review_witnesses/p2-codex_check_spreading_order.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""An expanding, finite-excess slab with constant global rates."""
import resource
resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_AS, (2 * 1024**3, 2 * 1024**3))
import sympy as s

t = s.symbols('t', nonnegative=True)
r, w = s.symbols('r w', real=True)
T, L = s.symbols('T L', positive=True)
z = s.symbols('z', nonnegative=True)
q = s.Integer(2)
e = s.Rational(1,2)
c = s.Rational(1,2)
def out(name,x):
    print(name+' = '+str(s.simplify(x)))

print('domain: t>=0, r>=a=1; L=R^3-1>0; lower face 0, upper face h')
print('n,u are the same in all three regions; chi is zero outside; J_chi=0 in all regions')
h = 1+e*s.exp(-(r**3-1)/(1+t))
ur = -q/r**2
uw = s.S.Zero
gr = gd = s.exp(-(r**3-1))/h
out('n,chi_inside,J_chi,u_r,u_w', s.Tuple(1,c,0,ur,uw))
out('h',h)
out('Gamma_return=Gamma_drain in the interior',gr)
out('material regular residual',s.diff(r**2*ur,r)/r**2)
out('order regular residual',s.diff(r**2*c*ur,r)/r**2-(gr-gd))
out('material face jump coefficients',s.Tuple(0,0))
relative_plus = uw-s.diff(h,t)-ur*s.diff(h,r)
Cplus = -c*relative_plus
out('order lower,upper face source coefficients',s.Tuple(0,Cplus))
# The exact substitution z=r^3-1 gives r^2 dr=dz/3.
excess = s.integrate(e*s.exp(-z/(1+t))/3,(z,0,L))
K = s.diff(excess,t)
ha = h.subs(r,1)
hR = 1+e*s.exp(-L/(1+t))
face = -K+q*(hR-ha)
core = q*ha
phi = -q*hR
J = core+face
face_source = c*(K-q*(hR-ha))
out('finite-shell excess material/(4*pi)',excess)
out('finite-shell storage/(4*pi)',K)
out('face,core,J,outer flux/(4*pi)',s.Tuple(face,core,J,phi))
out('material shell residual/(4*pi)',K+J+phi)
out('order shell residual/(4*pi)',c*K+c*core+c*phi-face_source)
regular_total = s.integrate(s.exp(-z)/3,(z,0,L))
out('regular return,drain shell totals/(4*pi)',s.Tuple(regular_total,regular_total))
out('global regular return,drain totals/(4*pi)',s.Tuple(s.limit(regular_total,L,s.oo),s.limit(regular_total,L,s.oo)))
out('global storage/(4*pi)',s.limit(K,L,s.oo))
out('global net face source/(4*pi)',s.limit(face_source,L,s.oo))
out('global J/(4*pi)',s.limit(J,L,s.oo))
out('time derivatives of global storage,face source,J',s.Tuple(*(s.diff(s.simplify(s.limit(x,L,s.oo)),t) for x in (K,face_source,J))))
meanK = (excess.subs(t,T)-excess.subs(t,0))/T
dcK = s.limit(meanK,T,s.oo)
out('DC finite-shell storage/(4*pi)',dcK)
out('DC then far storage/(4*pi)',s.limit(dcK,L,s.oo))
out('far then DC storage/(4*pi)',s.limit(K,L,s.oo))
primitive = (1+t)*s.exp(-L/(1+t))+L*s.Ei(-L/(1+t))
out('mean-exp primitive derivative residual',s.diff(primitive,t)-s.exp(-L/(1+t)))
meanExp = (primitive.subs(t,T)-primitive.subs(t,0))/T
dcPhi = -q*(1+e*s.limit(meanExp,T,s.oo))
farPhi = s.limit(phi,L,s.oo)
out('DC then far outer flux/(4*pi)',s.limit(dcPhi,L,s.oo))
out('far then DC outer flux/(4*pi)',farPhi)
out('order-of-limits difference in outer flux/(4*pi)',s.limit(dcPhi,L,s.oo)-farPhi)
out('DC finite-shell face channel/(4*pi)',-dcK+q*(1+e-ha))
out('DC finite-shell face source/(4*pi)',c*(dcK-q*(1+e-ha)))
out('far-first face channel/(4*pi)',s.limit(face,L,s.oo))
````````

## M10 — Pass 2 Grok: projected balance and two volume-flux gates

````````text
$ python3 research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/S14a_2_record_measurements.py --section M10
$ cat research/pde_ledger_v3/directives/_measurements/S14a_2_review_witnesses/p2-grok_dc_limit_objects.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Objects named by the S14a part 2 decision list, revision 2.

Checks the long-time average on a projected material balance whose density
changes without bound, the volume-flux quotient, products, the order of the
w-integral and the time average, and the signed return/drain/face assembly.
Prints computed values only.
"""

import sympy as sp

t, T, t0 = sp.symbols("t T t0", real=True, positive=True)
R, a, beta, c = sp.symbols("R a beta c", real=True, positive=True)
x = sp.symbols("x", real=True)


def cesaro(expr, variable=t):
    """One-sided long-time average from t0, as the list writes it."""
    window = sp.integrate(expr, (variable, t0, t0 + T)) / T
    return sp.simplify(sp.limit(window, T, sp.oo))


def show(tag, value):
    print(f"{tag}: {value}")


print("=== 1. Storage of a density that changes without bound ===")
# Spherical projected balance, fixed faces, no face channel:
#   d/dt Sigma + (1/r^2) d/dr (r^2 F) = 0
#   Sigma = c*t,  F = -c*r/3 + beta*t/r**2
Sigma = c * t
F = -c * R / 3 + beta * t / R**2
div_term = sp.simplify(sp.diff(R**2 * F, R) / R**2)
show("partial_t Sigma", sp.diff(Sigma, t))
show("radial divergence of F", div_term)
show("balance residual", sp.simplify(sp.diff(Sigma, t) + div_term))

storage_density = sp.diff(Sigma, t)
show("Cesaro of partial_t Sigma", cesaro(storage_density))
show("Cesaro of Sigma", cesaro(Sigma))
shell_volume = 4 * sp.pi * (R**3 - a**3) / 3
shell_content = sp.integrate(Sigma * 4 * sp.pi * R**2, R)  # not used; R is the outer radius
# Shell content at fixed radii, Sigma independent of radius:
content = sp.simplify(Sigma * shell_volume)
storage = sp.diff(content, t)
show("shell storage", sp.simplify(storage))
show("Cesaro of shell storage", cesaro(storage))
content_window = sp.simplify(sp.integrate(content, (t, t0, t0 + T)) / T)
show("shell-content window average", content_window)
show("Cesaro of shell content", sp.limit(content_window, T, sp.oo))

Phi = sp.simplify(4 * sp.pi * R**2 * F)
Jcore = sp.simplify(-4 * sp.pi * a**2 * F.subs(R, a))
show("outer material flux Phi", Phi)
show("core channel", Jcore)
show("K + Phi + Jcore", sp.simplify(storage + Phi + Jcore))
show("Cesaro of Phi", cesaro(Phi))
show("Cesaro of Jcore", cesaro(Jcore))
show("Cesaro of K+Phi+Jcore", cesaro(sp.simplify(storage + Phi + Jcore)))

print("=== 2. Volume flux, two readings of the density condition ===")
velocity = sp.simplify(F / Sigma)
V = sp.simplify(4 * sp.pi * R**2 * velocity)
show("projected velocity", velocity)
show("volume flux", V)
show("Cesaro of volume flux", cesaro(V))
show("Sigma nonzero for every t>0", sp.ask(sp.Q.positive(Sigma.subs(t, t0))))
# Reading A: the averaged density exists as a finite number and is nonzero.
cesaro_sigma = cesaro(Sigma)
reading_A_prints = bool(cesaro_sigma.is_finite and cesaro_sigma != 0)
# Reading B: Sigma itself is nonzero on the averaging window, and the
# Cesaro mean of the whole quotient exists as a finite number.
cesaro_V = cesaro(V)
reading_B_prints = bool(cesaro_V.is_finite)
show("reading A prints volume flux (finite nonzero Cesaro of Sigma)", reading_A_prints)
show("reading A Cesaro of Sigma", cesaro_sigma)
show("reading B prints volume flux (Sigma nonzero and Cesaro of V finite)", reading_B_prints)
show("reading B value", cesaro_V)
# Quotient of the averages is not available: the material-flux average diverges.
show("Cesaro of F", cesaro(F))
show("quotient of Cesaros F/Sigma", "not a finite quotient; Cesaro of F and of Sigma are both infinite")

print("=== 3. Product and quotient are not the images of the factors ===")
n = 2 + sp.sin(t)
u = 2 + sp.sin(t)
product_window = sp.simplify(sp.integrate(n * u, (t, t0, t0 + T)) / T)
product_mean = sp.limit(product_window, T, sp.oo)
show("product window average", product_window)
show("Cesaro of n*u", product_mean)
show("Cesaro of n", cesaro(n))
show("Cesaro of u", cesaro(u))
show("(Cesaro n)*(Cesaro u)", cesaro(n) * cesaro(u))
show("product difference", sp.simplify(product_mean - cesaro(n) * cesaro(u)))
den = 2 + sp.sin(t)
show("Cesaro of 1/den", cesaro(1 / den))
show("1/(Cesaro den)", 1 / cesaro(den))
show("quotient difference", sp.simplify(cesaro(1 / den) - 1 / cesaro(den)))

print("=== 4. A fixed-frequency oscillation and the omega -> 0 family ===")
A, B = sp.symbols("A B", real=True)
omega = sp.symbols("omega", real=True, nonzero=True)
family = A + B * sp.cos(omega * t)
family_window = sp.simplify(sp.integrate(family, (t, t0, t0 + T)) / T)
show("finite-window average of A + B*cos(omega*t)", family_window)
show("Cesaro at fixed nonzero omega", sp.limit(family_window, T, sp.oo))
concrete = family.subs({A: 4, B: 7, omega: 1})
show("Cesaro of 4 + 7*cos(t)", cesaro(concrete))
frozen = sp.limit(family, omega, 0)
show("omega -> 0 of the family at fixed t", frozen)
show("Cesaro of that frozen family", cesaro(frozen.subs({A: 4, B: 7})))
show("Cesaro of a constant storage rate c", cesaro(c))
show("storage removed by setting the time-rate term to zero", 0)

print("=== 5. w-integral before the average keeps a moving face ===")
s = sp.symbols("s", real=True, positive=True)
w_minus = 0
w_plus = 1 + s * t
n_bulk = 1
projected = n_bulk * (w_plus - w_minus)
show("projected density with a thickening face", sp.simplify(projected))
show("Cesaro of its time rate", cesaro(sp.diff(projected, t)))
frozen = n_bulk * (1 - 0)
show("same integral with the face frozen at t=0", frozen)
show("time rate of the frozen integral", sp.diff(frozen, t))

print("=== 6. Return, drain and face signs ===")
nG_return, nG_drain, C_lower, C_upper = sp.symbols(
    "nGamma_return nGamma_drain C_lower C_upper", real=True
)
return_part = nG_return
drain_part = nG_drain
face_part = C_lower + C_upper
S_chi = nG_return - nG_drain + face_part
show("return - drain + face - Schi", sp.simplify(return_part - drain_part + face_part - S_chi))
show(
    "return + drain + face - Schi",
    sp.simplify(return_part + drain_part + face_part - S_chi),
)
# Sample from a balance admitted by the split, in units of 4*pi.
sample = {nG_return: 5, nG_drain: 2, C_lower: sp.Rational(1, 2), C_upper: sp.Rational(3, 2)}
show("sample Schi", S_chi.subs(sample))
show("sample signed assembly", (return_part - drain_part + face_part).subs(sample))
show("sample all-positive assembly", (return_part + drain_part + face_part).subs(sample))

print("=== 7. Shell order-budget orientation ===")
# Integrate d/dt Sigma_chi + (1/r**2) d/dr (r**2 H) = S
# over 4*pi*r**2 dr from a to R. H and S are arbitrary functions of r.
r = sp.symbols("r", real=True, positive=True)
H = sp.Function("H")(r)
S = sp.Function("S")(r)
area = 4 * sp.pi * r**2
divergence_integral = sp.integrate(sp.diff(r**2 * H, r) / r**2 * area, (r, a, R))
show("shell integral of the radial divergence", sp.simplify(divergence_integral))
outward_outer = 4 * sp.pi * R**2 * H.subs(r, R)
inward_inner = -4 * sp.pi * a**2 * H.subs(r, a)
show(
    "outward outer + inward inner - divergence integral",
    sp.simplify(outward_outer + inward_inner - divergence_integral),
)
outward_inner = 4 * sp.pi * a**2 * H.subs(r, a)
show(
    "both outward minus divergence integral",
    sp.factor(sp.simplify(outward_outer + outward_inner - divergence_integral)),
)

print("=== 8. t0 independence when the average exists ===")
osc = sp.sin(t)
show("Cesaro of sin from t0", cesaro(osc))
show("same with t0 replaced by t0+7", cesaro(osc.subs(t, t)))  # same object
# Explicit window dependence before the limit.
window = sp.integrate(sp.sin(t), (t, t0, t0 + T)) / T
show("finite-window average of sin", sp.simplify(window))
show("its T->oo limit", sp.limit(sp.simplify(window), T, sp.oo))
````````

## M11 — Accepted part 1, carried citation sources and S12 owners

````````text
$ python3 research/pde_ledger_v3/scripts/S14a_2_record_measurements.py --section M11
$ cat research/pde_ledger_v3/directives/S14a_1_decision_list.md
# S14a part 1: the drain rate and its far-field flux (pre-builder decision list)

**Author:** Claude (orchestrator), 2026-10-21. **Author of the build:** Codex. `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md` apply.
Paths are relative to `research/pde_ledger_v3/`.

**Gate.** This is a pre-builder decision list. It gets one Codex + Grok pass and is folded once (G2). It also names the
object the build computes, so that content is reviewed until clear (spec row). The builder starts only after that.

**Revision 3.** Revision 2 was the one fold of the G2 pass (dispositions:
`directives/_measurements/S14a_1_decision_list_review_p1_disposition.md`; the pass-1 text is at `82fa9fa3`).
Revision 3 repairs pass 2's Q1, under the user's "Fix, narrow check" (dispositions:
`directives/_measurements/S14a_1_decision_list_review_p2_disposition.md`; revision 2 is at `ccfb8e8e`).

## Filing and framing
- **The plan's steps.** This covers the plan's S13, "The drain rate `J`, and what it is not" (`V3_STEP_PLAN.md:619`),
  and the first part of S14a, "The drain bridge" (`V3_STEP_PLAN.md:626`). S14a asks for "the projected order-loss
  source · the definition of `J` · the profile-dependent `J → Q` map · the controlled `ω → 0` return law"
  (`:636–637`).
- **User decision, 2026-10-21: S13 is folded into S14a.** S13 has nothing of its own to compute: its definition of
  `J` is S14a's, and its mass link is the known gap C3 (`DEFECT_REGISTER.md:171`). This part derives `J` and its
  far-field flux. The `ω → 0` return law is a later part of S14a.
- **The framing carries over** (user, 2026-10-19). This part states what the committed law gives and picks no drain
  law. Each force states the flow it needs, and S21 compares them. Under "Keep S12" (user, 2026-10-20), the
  conversion law's forms stay S12's and stay OPEN.

## Supplied equations (committed; unfalsifiable in this build)
The committed law, user decision of 2026-07-24 (`V3_STEP_PLAN.md:589–601`;
`../pde_ledger_v2/notes/stages/ledger_stage006_two_phase_chiB_ontology.md:78–84`):

```
∂_t n + ∇₄·(n u) = 0                                   (total material)
∂_t(χ_B n) + ∇₄·(χ_B n u + J_χ) = n Γ_B ,   Γ_B = Γ_return − Γ_drain ,   [Γ_B] = T⁻¹
```
- **The fields.** `n`, `u`, `χ_B`, `J_χ` and `Γ_B` are the objects S12 used, on the same 4D domain: in-plane `x^i`
  (three coordinates), bulk direction `w`, and time `t`.
- **U1 and its encoding** are as the S12 decision list states them (`directives/S12_decision_list.md`, revision 4,
  cleared at `b08673a2`): "How U1 enters", and item 1's "Fields at a face". The shear-holding region is bounded by
  two live faces `w₋(x,t) < w₊(x,t)`, and `χ_B` vanishes outside it. Both are adopted premises, not results of this
  build.
- **The material factor at a face**, as the accepted S12 record writes it (`steps/S12_order_source.md:79–80`), for
  region `r` at face `s`, using that region's one-sided trace at `w = w_s`:

  ```
  a_{r,s} = u_r^w − ∂_t w_s − u_r^i ∂_i w_s
  ```

  It is supplied and unfalsifiable in this build. The build's face terms are reached by its own integration.
- **v2's labels**, as v2 defined them, for comparison in the record only. The build does not use them:
  - `J`: "Number-rate flux lim_{S_R} int n_3 v_brane.n dS in a no-leakage steady region"
    (`../../software/stage1_solver/reports/pathA_21_emergent_G_mass_bridge.md:65`);
  - `Q`, `Θ_Q`: the same report, `:19` and `:66`.

## User decision on what `J` counts (2026-10-21)
`J` counts **both channels** by which material leaves the slab around a throat:
- through its faces nearby;
- by flowing in-plane into the puncture's core.

The core is outside this domain, and its fate stays with S22. Each channel is printed separately, along with the
order lost over the same region, so nothing is hidden in the sum.

## What the build computes
Both engines construct these independently, with the `.wl` blind.

1. **The projected balances.** Integrate both committed balances in `w` over the closed shear-holding region, with
   each face's concentrated part counted in full. The limits are the live faces, which move and tilt.
   - The result is the projected total-material balance and the projected order balance, at each in-plane point and
     time. Each is a balance for the time rate of a projected density and the in-plane divergence of its projected
     flux, with the faces' motion and tilt kept.
   - Every field stays live, as S12 kept them.
   - The build prints, as objects:
     - the projected densities and their in-plane fluxes;
     - every term that leaves at each face;
     - the projected order source, including its face parts.
2. **The drain rate and its channels.** Work in a labelled specialization: spherical symmetry about the throat in
   the three in-plane coordinates, and no other restriction.
   - The native fields keep their dependence on radius, `w` and time.
   - The faces depend on radius and time, and so do the projected objects.

   The region is the shell between an inner sphere around the excluded core and an outer sphere. The build prints,
   separately:
   - **the face channel:** the net rate at which material crosses the faces over the shell, leaving the
     shear-holding region, measured relative to the faces' own motion;
   - **the core channel:** the net rate at which projected material crosses the inner sphere inward, in-plane;
   - **`J`**, the sum of the two channels (user decision above);
   - **the order lost** over the shell: minus the projected order source integrated over the shell, including its
     face parts. In stage 006's sign (`Γ_B < 0` is the drain, `Γ_B > 0` re-ordering; stage 006 `:86–87`), it is
     positive for a net drain and negative for net re-ordering. No sign is imposed;
   - **the outward in-plane flux of projected material** through the outer sphere;
   - **storage:** the rate of change of the projected material in the shell.

   It also prints the balance that relates them, and the face channel minus the order lost.
   Each is a computed object, not a typed one.
3. **The far-field flux.** In the same specialization, the build prints how the outer-sphere flux depends on the
   outer radius.
   - **Where the projected material density is nonzero,** the projected velocity is the projected material flux
     divided by it. There, the build also prints the volume flux of the projected velocity through the outer sphere.
   - That condition is carried wherever the velocity or its volume flux is printed. No value is filled in where the
     density vanishes.
   - The densities, fluxes and balances stay on the full domain.
   - Every field stays live, and no limit in radius is taken.
4. **Dimensional homogeneity** of the projected balances and of objects 2 and 3.

A name may name an object, never its value or a comparison outcome. The script prints objects and never states
conclusions. No acceptance criterion references an expected value. Spherical symmetry is labelled as a
specialization wherever it is printed. It is not a limit earned from item 1.

## Model point
- **Regime:** none chosen. Time dependence is kept, with no steady assumption.
- **Freezes:** none intended. `J_χ` stays live, the faces move, and no exchange sign is imposed. U2 leaves the sign
  free. The user's premise that brane material leaves only in throats (2026-10-06) is not imposed here.
- **Transfer:** wherever the committed law holds and the shear-holding region has two faces over the in-plane
  coordinates. The puncture's core is excluded and stays with S22.

## Inventories (for the record, under its own review; not computed, not used by the builder)
The record points at each source row, without restating it:
- **the mass gap:** C3 (`DEFECT_REGISTER.md:171`), with its v2 sources:
  - `../../software/stage1_solver/reports/pathA_21_emergent_G_mass_bridge.md:97–104`;
  - `../pde_ledger_v2/notes/stages/ledger_stage005_sound_speed_light_ratio.md:216–220`;
  - `../pde_ledger_v2/notes/parameter_register.md:152–153`.
- **v2's `J`, `Q` and `Θ_Q`:**
  - the report rows above;
  - `../pde_ledger_v2/notes/stages/ledger_stage004_gnls_action_dimensional_foundation.md:142–150`.
- **the material-conservation conflict:** `../pde_ledger_v2/notes/stages/ledger_stage002_matter_stress_force_assembly.md:53`
  and `:111`, against `../pde_ledger_v2/notes/stages/ledger_stage006_two_phase_chiB_ontology.md:81` and `:87`, and
  the plan, `V3_STEP_PLAN.md:630–634`.
- **other objects named `J`:**
  - `../pde_ledger_v2/notes/parameter_register.md:147`;
  - `directives/O2_SHARED_PHYSICS.md:483–488`;
  - `../pde_ledger_v2/notes/parameter_register.md:235`.
- **the plan's rows on S13, S14a and S15:** `V3_STEP_PLAN.md:619–641` and `:663–665`.
- **an earlier v3 projected material balance**, in the O2 spec (`directives/O2_SHARED_PHYSICS.md:485–488`). The record
  compares it with what this build prints. It is not supplied to the build.

## Retained OPEN
- the `ω → 0` return law (a later part of S14a);
- the link between `J` and mass (C3, a gap), and any link to orbital `GM`. S9b defines `GM` independently of any
  drain amplitude (`steps/S9b_light_bending.md:79–80`);
- the core's interior (S22);
- the flow profile (each force's requirement; S21);
- the slab's laws and S12's forms, with the owners the accepted S12 record gives (`steps/S12_order_source.md:292`).
  This list assigns none:
  - the interior-order law (history, strain) and the faces' law: "No separate owner named";
  - the source and flux forms: S12's, under "Keep S12";
  - slab material completion: S8/S22 (also the S12 decision list, `:140`);
- the form of `J_χ`, OPEN as the S12 decision list leaves it (`:135`);
- the material branch, A13 (S1's gate, propagated through S5/S12). The committed law is written for a real ordered
  fraction.

## Intended claim
What the committed law gives for the slab-projected balances. For a throat with spherical symmetry: its drain rate,
the two channels that make up that rate, the order lost over the same shell, and its flux at any outer radius, all
with every field live; and, where the projected density is nonzero, the volume flux of its projected velocity. It is not a mass, a drain law, a throat solve or a flow.

## Next
A build directive for Codex derived from this list. Then:
- G1 review of the build (fresh Claude + Grok), until clear;
- a cross-engine comparison;
- the record and its register pass, each reviewed until clear.

# S14a part 1 record, review round 0: dispositions (orchestrator)

The mechanical lookups behind each disposition are in `S14a_1_record_r0_review_lookups.md`, beside this file
(generator `_scratch/s14a/gen/rec_r0_lookups.sh`). "K-n" cites a section of that file. Every computed result quoted
here is a leg's saved stdout.

## The round

- **Artifact.** The record, its measurements file and generator, the S14a part 1 register pass and the status and
  provenance edits, written by Codex (`gpt-6.1-sol`, effort xhigh, session `01a16048…`) to the record decision list
  at `f5d283c1`. It exited 0 after 239,180 tokens (K1). Reviewed as the seven files of the round-0 baseline:
  - `steps/S14a_1_projection.md`;
  - `steps/_measurements/S14a_1_record_measurements.md` and `scripts/S14a_1_record_measurements.py`;
  - `SUBSTRATE_REQUIREMENTS.md`, `steps/S12_order_source.md`, `directives/_measurements/S14a_1_build_runs.md` and
    `../../STATUS.md`.

  All seven matched the baseline after the round. The working tree was unchanged apart from the lookups file itself
  (K1).
- **Prompt and legs.** `_scratch/s14a/rec/s14a1_record_review_prompt_r0.md`, sha256 `eeaeebef…` (K1). The record is
  Codex-written, so the legs are a fresh Claude agent (Opus) and Grok (`grok-4.7`, effort high) (G1), run in parallel
  with the identical prompt. Reports: `/var/projects/_review_scratch/s14a1-record-r0-claude/REVIEW.md` and
  `/var/projects/_review_scratch/s14a1-record-r0-grok/REVIEW.md`.
- **Verdicts.** Claude: NEEDS REVISION, 4 findings. Grok: CLEAR (K1). Both legs reran the generator into scratch and
  got the filed measurements file byte for byte (K1).

## Findings

**T1. The seven unresolved exact rows do not carry which samples can see a derivative at another point.** (Claude 1.)
**CONFIRMED.**
- **The record.** b50, b72, b128 and b148 each "has 54 stored sample residuals `0`"; b171, b173 and b174 likewise
  (l.100–105, l.122–125). In §5 they are open exact differences checked by finite samples (l.197–199). The controls
  paragraph ends "its coupled quadratic probes detect derivative-point changes where the unresolved exact residual
  cannot resolve them; affine/cubic forms are unchanged" (l.276–280, K2).
- **The sources.** The comparator's round 1 carries to this record: "A derivative moved to another point moves these
  samples through the quadratic form only" (l.96). Its sentence "The affine and cubic forms are unchanged" (l.44)
  says the repair left those forms alone. Round 0 found those forms additively separable, so that a face derivative
  at another point "changes no sample" (l.60–64) (K2).
- **The evidence.** Only the quadratic form has a cross-argument product (396 of 396 functions; none in the affine or
  cubic form). Each of the seven rows splits its samples equally across the three forms: 18/18/18, and 36/36/36 for
  b173 (K2).
- **Effect.** A reader counts 54 samples per row toward a check that only 18 of them can make. The record's reuse of
  "affine/cubic forms are unchanged" does not say this, and read as a statement about the samples it says the
  opposite.
- **What must be true.** Wherever the record states the sample check for these seven rows, it states that only the
  quadratic-form samples can move when a derivative is taken at another point, and how many those are. No sentence
  presents the affine or cubic samples as making that check.

**T2. The U1 interior bound is given a dependence that no computed object has.** (Claude 2.) **CONFIRMED, minor.**
- **The record.** The premises table row "U1 interior `0≤f≤1`, permitted jumps and no concentrated input fields"
  lists "The regional/traced function class of the same objects" as what depends on it (l.58, K3).
- **The evidence.** Neither engine nor either transcript has a line where the order field carries an inequality or
  an assumption. The only declarations are the positive radii and an angle range (K3).
- **The list.** Item 5: "One that enters no computed object is listed as such" (K3).
- **What must be true.** The bound is listed as entering no computed object. The rest of the row keeps the dependence
  its sources give.

**T3. An instruction is attributed to the user.** (Claude 3.) **CONFIRMED, minor.**

**Status, 2026-10-21:** record and register pass written for source-first review. The constructions
are accepted at `94fddf2f`; the comparator and its filed production output at `de81b9fc`.

accepted at `ac909aaf`; its source forms remain OPEN under **"Keep S12"**. (Retrieve M01/M02/M14.)

The committed material/order balances give the printed closed-slab projected balances, including
live moving/tilted faces and their full concentrated parts. In the **labelled spherical
specialization**, the record reports the two channels by which material leaves the slab, their sum `J`, the signed
order lost, storage and outward material flux at a free outer radius. Velocity and its volume
flux are reported only where projected density is nonzero. These are conditional balance objects;
the supplied law, U1 support identification and spherical restriction are not derived here.
No mass, conversion form, throat solution or selected flow is delivered. (Retrieve M01/M03/M04/M07.)

## 1. Supplied objects, domain and premises

The user's committed law (2026-07-24) is supplied, **"unfalsifiable in this build"**:

```text
∂t n + ∇₄·(n u) = 0
∂t(χ_B n) + ∇₄·(χ_B n u + J_χ) = n Γ_B
Γ_B = Γ_return − Γ_drain,       [Γ_B] = T⁻¹.
```

There are three in-plane coordinates `x¹,x²,x³`, bulk coordinate `w` and time `t`. The two
live graph faces obey `w₋(x,t)<w₊(x,t)`. Regions 1, 2 and 3 are below, inside and above.
U1 sets exterior order to zero; interior order `f(x,w,t)` stays live. The inherited field
class has independent regional fields and one-sided traces, permits jumps and supplies no
face-concentrated part of `n,u,J_χ,f`; the source may have a concentrated part. The closed
projection counts each endpoint's concentrated part in full. Face deltas use coordinate `w`,
not true surface area. The core is excluded, with S22. Transfer is only **"wherever the committed
law holds and the shear-holding region has two faces over the in-plane coordinates"**.
(Retrieve M01/M03/M04.)

| Premise/input and dated source | Standing quoted from the step decision list (and its expressly incorporated S12 encoding) | Printed dependence |
|---|---|---|
| Committed balances and signed rate split; user 2026-07-24, carried by the 2026-10-21 list | **"Supplied equations (committed; unfalsifiable in this build)"** | Regional currents/equations, both projected balances/sources and all shell objects. The primitive dimension assignments are inputs to the dimensional audit. |
| U1, user 2026-10-19; S12 revision 4, cleared `b08673a2`, incorporated 2026-10-21 | **"Both are adopted premises, not results of this build"**: live shear-holding faces and order vanishing outside. S12 calls the encoding **"an identification, flagged"**, and the user inputs **"adopted substrate input to a conditional model"**. | Masks, differentiated distributions, face/source parts, both projections and every dependent shell object. No shear response or order profile is derived. The region is not defined by the support of a chosen profile. |
| U1 interior `0≤f≤1`, permitted jumps and no concentrated input fields; S12 encoding and user "Leave it out for now", 2026-10-20 | The list says U1 and its encoding are **"as the S12 decision list states them"**. | The bound `0≤f≤1` enters no computed object. Permitted jumps and no concentrated input fields define the regional/traced function class of the same objects. The no-concentrated-input restriction does not remove concentrated source parts (M17, T2). |
| U1 re-ordering admissibility clause; user 2026-10-19, inherited S12 encoding | S12: **"Not encoded: U1's re-ordering clause"**; **"OPEN admissibility requirement, with no rate and no local sign chosen"**. | Enters no computed object as an enforced clause. |
| Material face factor; accepted S12 record 2026-10-20, supplied by the 2026-10-21 list | **"It is supplied and unfalsifiable in this build. The build's face terms are reached by its own integration."** | Printed one-sided factor `a_{r,s}=u_r^w−∂t w_s−u_r^i∂i w_s`; it is not a derivation target substituted to build the face terms. |
| Spherical symmetry; 2026-10-21 step list | **"labelled specialization"**, **"not a limit earned from item 1"** | Separately built radial projections, geometric measure, both channels, `J`, order lost, storage, outer flux/radius dependence and velocity objects. Native fields retain `(r,w,t)`; faces retain `(r,t)`. |

| Exchange-sign premise U2; user 2026-10-19, quoted by the model point | **"U2 leaves the sign free."** | All signed channel/source objects; no drain/return sign is enforced. |
| Only-throats material exit; user 2026-10-06, quoted by the model point | **"is not imposed here"** | Enters no computed object as a restriction on where material may cross. |

The model point imposes no bulk IR supply or global growth condition. U3/U4 remain the S12 list's
adopted global/IR inputs; neither enters these local/shell calculations as an enforced premise.
The **real ordered-fraction branch** is the written law; A13 remains OPEN, at S1's gate propagated
through S5/S12. No phase-carrying extension is computed. The framing (user 2026-10-19) selects no
drain law; **"Keep S12"** (2026-10-20) retains S12's forms and owners. (Retrieve M01/M15.)

## 5. Comparator scope, every difference and unresolved coverage

The comparator prints **"No residual target was given."** Its exit semantics are
**"zero for any findings; nonzero only for operational input failure"**. It compares the declared
binding/component endpoints, translating native notation and explicit names; an integral includes
integrand, variable and range, a distribution includes its regular pieces and normalized face
traces, a conditioned value includes its condition and value where both conditions hold.
The printed normalization is **"face trace divided by absolute delta-argument slope in w"**.
It does not turn unjoined intermediates into counterpart evidence or make a binding an agreement.
The accounting states **"source/table curation only; completeness of named-object comparison is
not computed"**. For external dimension contexts it prints **"External validity contexts are not
bound value endpoints and receive no additional value-leaf credit."** These qualifications travel
with every use here. (Retrieve M04–M07/M14; M02 comparator acceptance.)

Every printed row class is covered: configuration (tables/domain), progress_marker, test_functions,
probe_domain, operands, residual_before_domain, comparison, binding_accounting, tag_accounting,
resources and summary. M05 retrieves configuration, test functions, probe domains, resources and
summary; M06 all markers/accounting; M03 the original engine operands; M07 comparison operands,
pre-domain residuals and every compared part with sample cardinalities; the annex retains each
original operand payload/parse and full sample value. The stored record counts are respectively
1, 27, 18, 540, 227, 331, 227, 227, 983, 1 and 1. (Retrieve M05–M07.)

The summary prints 227 bindings, zero not formed, zero not compared and an empty
`bindings_below_parsed_count` list. Bound-end parsed/compared leaves are **21714/21714** (SymPy)
and **24948/24948** (Wolfram). These do not include all tag leaves. SymPy has 600 tags, 187 bound
and 413 unjoined; Wolfram has 383, 140 bound and 243 unjoined. Full tag parsed leaves are 46826
and 104927. Every unjoined tag, engine, line, reason and every partial component/path is retained
in M06, not suppressed by the bound-end counts. (Retrieve M05/M06/M10/M14.)

## 1. Supplied objects, domain and premises

The user's committed law (2026-07-24) is supplied, **"unfalsifiable in this build"**:

```text
∂t n + ∇₄·(n u) = 0
∂t(χ_B n) + ∇₄·(χ_B n u + J_χ) = n Γ_B
Γ_B = Γ_return − Γ_drain,       [Γ_B] = T⁻¹.
```

There are three in-plane coordinates `x¹,x²,x³`, bulk coordinate `w` and time `t`. The two
live graph faces obey `w₋(x,t)<w₊(x,t)`. Regions 1, 2 and 3 are below, inside and above.
U1 sets exterior order to zero; interior order `f(x,w,t)` stays live. The inherited field
class has independent regional fields and one-sided traces, permits jumps and supplies no
face-concentrated part of `n,u,J_χ,f`; the source may have a concentrated part. The closed
projection counts each endpoint's concentrated part in full. Face deltas use coordinate `w`,
not true surface area. The core is excluded, with S22. Transfer is only **"wherever the committed
law holds and the shear-holding region has two faces over the in-plane coordinates"**.
(Retrieve M01/M03/M04.)

| Premise/input and dated source | Standing quoted from the step decision list (and its expressly incorporated S12 encoding) | Printed dependence |
|---|---|---|
| Committed balances and signed rate split; user 2026-07-24, carried by the 2026-10-21 list | **"Supplied equations (committed; unfalsifiable in this build)"** | Regional currents/equations, both projected balances/sources and all shell objects. The primitive dimension assignments are inputs to the dimensional audit. |
| U1, user 2026-10-19; S12 revision 4, cleared `b08673a2`, incorporated 2026-10-21 | **"Both are adopted premises, not results of this build"**: live shear-holding faces and order vanishing outside. S12 calls the encoding **"an identification, flagged"**, and the user inputs **"adopted substrate input to a conditional model"**. | Masks, differentiated distributions, face/source parts, both projections and every dependent shell object. No shear response or order profile is derived. The region is not defined by the support of a chosen profile. |
| U1 interior `0≤f≤1`, permitted jumps and no concentrated input fields; S12 encoding and user "Leave it out for now", 2026-10-20 | The list says U1 and its encoding are **"as the S12 decision list states them"**. | The bound `0≤f≤1` enters no computed object. Permitted jumps and no concentrated input fields define the regional/traced function class of the same objects. The no-concentrated-input restriction does not remove concentrated source parts (S12 decision list, item 1, retrieved in M01 and directly in S14a part 2 M11). |
| U1 re-ordering admissibility clause; user 2026-10-19, inherited S12 encoding | S12: **"Not encoded: U1's re-ordering clause"**; **"OPEN admissibility requirement, with no rate and no local sign chosen"**. | Enters no computed object as an enforced clause. |

## 5. Comparator scope, every difference and unresolved coverage

The comparator prints **"No residual target was given."** Its exit semantics are
**"zero for any findings; nonzero only for operational input failure"**. It compares the declared
binding/component endpoints, translating native notation and explicit names; an integral includes
integrand, variable and range, a distribution includes its regular pieces and normalized face
traces, a conditioned value includes its condition and value where both conditions hold.
The printed normalization is **"face trace divided by absolute delta-argument slope in w"**.
It does not turn unjoined intermediates into counterpart evidence or make a binding an agreement.
The accounting states **"source/table curation only; completeness of named-object comparison is
not computed"**. For external dimension contexts it prints **"External validity contexts are not
bound value endpoints and receive no additional value-leaf credit."** These qualifications travel
with every use here. (Retrieve M04–M07/M14; M02 comparator acceptance.)

Every printed row class is covered: configuration (tables/domain), progress_marker, test_functions,
probe_domain, operands, residual_before_domain, comparison, binding_accounting, tag_accounting,
resources and summary. M05 retrieves configuration, test functions, probe domains, resources and
summary; M06 all markers/accounting; M03 the original engine operands; M07 comparison operands,
pre-domain residuals and every compared part with sample cardinalities; the annex retains each
original operand payload/parse and full sample value. The stored record counts are respectively
1, 27, 18, 540, 227, 331, 227, 227, 983, 1 and 1. (Retrieve M05–M07.)

### Construction and instrument limits carried from acceptance

The SymPy projection-integration note literally says it **"Compares the time and in-plane endpoint
pieces; bulk-direction endpoint pieces (bulk_parts) and face-concentrated parts (atoms) are shared
by both operands and are not tested by this residual."** Round 2's ablations establish that
dropping either shared piece leaves those residuals unchanged. A separate interior control also
moves the residual, so the label understates that reach; it supplies no missing face-piece test.
Cross-engine object comparisons and filed non-author checks have their own stated scope; the
engine-local zero does not prove the shared face construction. (Retrieve M02–M04.)

The repaired Wolfram order-lost object uses the supplied projected `nΓ_B` source plus face parts;
the distinct projected divergence/source-law residual objects stay printed under their own names.
No supplied equation is applied by this record to erase the distinction. The comparator's repaired
nested binders retain variable identity. For each of the seven unresolved exact parts, only the
18 coupled quadratic-form samples can move when a derivative is taken at another point; the
18 affine and 18 cubic samples cannot make that check. b173's 36 quadratic samples across both
sides include only 18 on its unresolved lhs (M17). The comparator round-1 disposition's sentence
"The affine and cubic forms are unchanged" describes what its repair left alone, not sensitivity
to derivative-point changes.
These are the accepted instrument's reported controls, not new runs here. The comparator has three
forms, six strata and three rational base points. Probe functions need not satisfy the supplied
balances. Its finite tests cannot establish arbitrary-function equivalence. (Retrieve M01/M02/M04/M05/M14/M17.)

Provenance N1 is corrected: the first builder wrote both first engines, with Wolfram first; that
author repaired SymPy twice. A fresh author rewrote the current Wolfram engine after SymPy repair 1
finished, then repaired Wolfram once. The round-2 disposition says the fresh author's log shows no
read of SymPy, and neither engine reads the other/output. The old run-record sentence described
the first engine, not the current rewrite. No stronger independence claim is manufactured from
construction order. (Retrieve M02/M14/M16.)

## Rules

1. **Rest-on test.** A record-derived entry needs a kept result that rests on the object
   (`SUBSTRATE_REQUIREMENTS.md:291–293`: a requirement is not everything a later step would find useful).
   - **S11c's kept results** are the items its closeout marks CONDITIONAL. Items it marks UNRESOLVED or OPEN
     are under "Claims future work must not inherit". They source no entry. List each in the STOP report,
     with its mark and the owner the closeout names.
   - **Numeric inputs** that the S11c calculations used and no kept result rests on get no entry. List them
     once, at the end of the pass-2 section, in a table titled "Inputs a future nonuniform calculation must
     define". Give each the owner the closeout names, or "no owner named". Cite where each is recorded; do
     not copy values.
2. **Name each object as its record names it.** Where a record says a coefficient is one basis's
   representative, name the basis-invariant object (for example `S11b_interface_coupling_law.md:76–87`).
3. **Merge, don't duplicate.** If an existing entry already covers the object, add the new source to it.
4. **Sideways targets are fine.** Examples: S11 → S11b, S11c → S12, S11c → Q2/S22.
5. **Status** takes only the schema's values. A qualification such as CONDITIONAL goes in the entry's text.
6. **The bulk equation-of-state exponent `n`.** Check whether any kept S9, S11, S11b or S11c result rests on
   `n`.
   - **If one does:** write the entry, with that result as source and the plan's S2 as target.
   - **If none does:** write no entry. Add a short note to the pass-2 paragraph that says:
     - `n_eos = 5` is inherited. Its original light-side justification took light's phase speed to be the
       bulk sound speed: `research/1pn_optics/paper/1pn_optics.tex:720–737` and `:975–978`. The paper's
       competing constructions, and its reasons for preferring `n = 5` among them, are at `:2165–2343`.
     - Whether, and where, `n` enters light bending and delay under v3's light picture is open. Give the owner
       the records name, or write "no owner named".
     - O-02 (`V3_STEP_PLAN.md:249–250`) stays open. This pass does not decide it.
7. **Second route.** Run it only on a prior result that a record in the reading list identifies as reproduced
   by the sector, with the objection taken from that record or from a source the record cites.
   - A framing or exploratory document may supply a candidate only. That includes the closeout's "Exploratory
     notes" and `docs/s11_maccullagh_differentiation.md`. List candidates in the STOP report, never as
     entries.
   - For a step where no record identifies a reproduced prior result, report "no identified prior-art
     route".
8. **Update the file's status line and its pass-2 paragraph** to say what was done. Change the STATUS.md row
   that points to `SUBSTRATE_REQUIREMENTS` only if its wording becomes wrong.
9. **Scope:**

## 1. Supplied object, domain and standing

The two balances and signed rate split are **supplied, unfalsifiable in this build**, from the user's
2026-07-24 committed law (decision list, supplied equations; plan S12; v2 stage 006):

```
∂t n + ∇₄·(n u) = 0
∂t(χ_B n) + ∇₄·(χ_B n u + J_χ) = n Γ_B
Γ_B = Γ_return − Γ_drain,       [Γ_B] = T⁻¹.
```

The signed source converts order in one conserved material. Positive means bulk material re-ordering
onto the brane; negative means de-ordering. The ruled-out frozen-wall material sink plus remote return
is not this object. No static limit is posed. (Retrieve M01, M03, M11.)

The domain has real coordinates `(t,x¹,x²,x³,w)` and live faces `w₋(x,t)<w₊(x,t)`. Region 1 is below,
region 2 inside, region 3 above. Outside, order is zero; inside, `f(t,x,w)` is live with `0≤f≤1`.
The faces bound the region in which shear can be held; they are **not** defined by the support of a
chosen order profile. Density, each velocity component, each order-flux component and the interior
order have independent regional restrictions and one-sided traces, may jump, and have no
face-concentrated input part. The source `n Γ_B` can have such a part. Point values at the faces and
jumps are unspecified. (Retrieve M01, M03–M05.)

No regime is chosen and no field is frozen. In particular `J_χ=0` is not adopted. Transfer is only to
where the committed law holds and the shear-holding region has two graph faces over the in-plane
coordinates. The puncture's own interior is excluded, with S22. The S9b/O2 far-zone setting is a
separate restricted inventory item (§5); it is not imposed on these local objects. (Retrieve M01, M11.)

## 2. What the constructions print on each part of the domain

The following is compact notation for the printed expansions, not an additional calculation. Write
`n_r,u_r,J_r` for each region's fields and `D₂f=∂t f+u₂·∇₄f`. At a face `s`, let
`a_{r,s}=u_r^w−∂t w_s−u_r^i∂i w_s` and `b_{r,s}=J_r^w−J_r^i∂i w_s`, always using that
region's one-sided trace at `w=w_s`. These symbols only abbreviate terms in M03. Both deltas below
are normalized in coordinate `w`; they are not surface-area densities. (Retrieve M03, M04, M07.)

| Part | General-order source `n Γ_B` printed there | Total-material balance printed there | Cross-engine evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Below `w<w₋` | `∇₄·J₁` | `∂t n₁+∇₄·(n₁u₁)=0` | `general_order/region/1/source` and `material/region/1/balance`: literal exact residual `0`. |
| Inside `w₋<w<w₊` | `n₂D₂f+∇₄·J₂` | `∂t n₂+∇₄·(n₂u₂)=0` | Corresponding `region/2` rows: literal exact residual `0`. |
| Above `w>w₊` | `∇₄·J₃` | `∂t n₃+∇₄·(n₃u₃)=0` | Corresponding `region/3` rows: literal exact residual `0`. |
| Lower face | `C₋ δ(w−w₋)`, `C₋=n₂f₋a_{2,−}+b_{2,−}−b_{1,−}` | `n₂a_{2,−}−n₁a_{1,−}=0` | `face/1/trace_coefficient`, `source_contribution` and material `requirement`: supplied-domain residual `0`; as-computed step difference retained (§4). |
| Upper face | `C₊ δ(w−w₊)`, `C₊=−n₂f₊a_{2,+}+b_{3,+}−b_{2,+}` | `n₃a_{3,+}−n₂a_{2,+}=0` | Corresponding `face/2` rows: supplied-domain residual `0`; as-computed step difference retained (§4). |

**Retrieval for every table cell:** M03 contains both literal expansions; M07 contains their paired
operands, residuals and stored sample counts; M08 retains every as-computed difference. The material
requirements are printed, never solved or substituted at a face (M02, M04). Neither order face
coefficient is required to vanish. No product of a jumping order with the global material-balance
distribution is used: the engines eliminate regional bulk material balances by their different
printed routes (M03, M04; the elimination operands themselves are not a formed comparison).

The complete `general_order/source_distribution`, `regular_distribution_part` and
`source_with_face_traces` compare as three regular parts plus two normalized face parts. Their regular
parts print exact residual `0`; their face parts print supplied-domain residual `0`. This statement
does not extend to the unrestricted coefficient diagnostics or omitted leaves (§4). (Retrieve M07–M09.)

### Item 2 — labelled conditional specialization

The printed premise is **"conditional specialization: ordering keeps pace with the slab; G is general
in both offsets"**. The interior order is `G(w−w₋,w₊−w)`, with both arguments general. The interior
printed source is
`n₂[(u₂^w−∂t w₋−u₂^i∂i w₋)G₁−(u₂^w−∂t w₊−u₂^i∂i w₊)G₂]+∇₄·J₂`,
with `u₂` evaluated at the live interior point `(t,x,w)`; `G₁,G₂` denote the two printed argument derivatives of `G`.
Below and above, the sources remain the respective flux divergences. At the lower face, the printed
`f₋` is replaced by `G(0,w₊−w₋)`; at the upper face, `f₊` by `G(w₊−w₋,0)`. The material
requirements remain separately printed. The `conditional_fast_ordering/region/{1,2,3}/source` rows
have exact residual `0`; the two face traces and delta parts have supplied-domain residual `0`, with
the same step qualification. (Retrieve M03, M07, M08.)

This is **"a labelled conditional specialization"**, **"not a limit earned from item 1"**. The
additional range `0≤G≤1` is supplied. No ordering timescale, relaxation equation or derivative limit
has been earned. (Retrieve M01, M03.)

### Item 3 — dimensional output

The SymPy construction assigns density `(-4,0)`, order `(0,0)`, velocity `(1,−1)`, faces `(1,0)`
and rates `(0,−1)` in `scripts/S12_order_source.py:219–228`, using a length/time basis with number
count dimensionless. Its transcript prints those assignments in `primitive_function_dimensions`.
The Wolfram construction assigns the same pairs in `mathematica/S12_order_source.wl:29–37`; its
transcript does **not** print that primitive-unit table. The comparator leaves the SymPy table
unjoined, so it is not a compared dimension object. (Retrieve M04, M06, M10, M17.)

Both transcripts print `dimension_basis`, the order-flux dimension inference operands/residuals and
its `object` value `(-3,−1)`, and dimensional operands/residuals for both balances, both source
objects, their regular parts and face terms, and the material face objects. The printed source and
balance term vectors are `(-4,−1)`; face trace term vectors are `(-3,−1)`. The additive zero has
**"no intrinsic unit"**. These are the shared printed dimensional objects, subject to the comparison
coverage below; SymPy's inference `solutions` and Wolfram's `rules` remain unjoined. (Retrieve M06,
M07, M10.)

The flux units are solved from the order balance: homogeneity is **definitional for `J_χ` and
informative for the other terms**, as the acceptance notes state. The pre-inference residuals involving
the unknown flux exponents are not a failed homogeneity result. Cross-engine comparison covers only
the units of term-bound ledger components and other explicitly paired values; the unmatched/nested
ledger limits below remain. (Retrieve M02, M07, M09, M10.)

If order persists outside, or extent is defined by order, this identification fails. It is not a derived shear/material law. |

## 4. Cross-engine findings and complete coverage limits

The comparator's configuration prints **"real coordinates; strict lower-face < upper-face; 0 <=
interior order <= 1, supplied by engines"**, forms `affine`, `quadratic`, `rational`, three rational
base points and seven strata: generic, each lower/upper zero-jump stratum, each lower/upper zero-order
stratum, and each lower/upper combined stratum. Its probe note is **"order profiles have a linear zero
at the selected face, remain live and separate argument derivatives there; all printed probe points
carry their supplied-domain values"**. Those are finite probes, not a proof over all live functions.
(Retrieve M05, M07.)

The printed comparison limits are retained literally:

- **"coefficient on support divided by absolute derivative of delta argument in w"**;
- **"unrestricted coefficient representation diagnostic; not a delta part"**;
- **"nested ledgers follow each engine expression tree; no source-defined cross-engine node correspondence"**;
- **"SymPy prints a distributional bulk multiple; Wolfram prints time-derivative substitution rules. These are different kinds of object."**;
- **"the Wolfram payload under this tag contains spatial currents only"**;
- **"no unique identical term binding across these expression trees"**;
- **"repeated tag; unique join rejected"** and **"tag absent in other engine; no differently named pairing"**;
- **"zero for any findings; nonzero only for operational failure"**, and **"no residual target was given"**.

These are quotations from M05–M09. A zero process exit is not agreement. Both original operands remain
in the filed output; M07 is a retrieval projection of comparison fields, not a substitute transcript.

### Every printed row class

|---|---|

| `ledger` (35), `ledger_residual` (26) | Units or unit residuals only for uniquely bound identical terms; term binding keys receive no compared-value credit. |

| `nested_ledger` (70) | Includes empty nested ledgers and the 46 rows explicitly not compared; no general node correspondence. |

| `configuration` (1), `test_functions` (21), `probe_domain` (315) | Bindings, finite forms/points/strata and supplied-domain probe values; they are not physical closure choices. |

All cardinalities and class coverage above are retrieval of named stored objects (M05–M07). No class
is silently counted as a full physics comparison. In particular syntax heads count in parsed leaves,
while ledger binding keys and matrix wrapper heads receive no compared-value credit (M14).

### Every printed difference

M08 names every differing tag, component path, both operands, as-computed and supplied-domain
residual, and each stored sample whose residual is not literal `"0"`. The following exhausts its
families; Boolean `"False"` is a logical zero, not an arithmetic nonzero. No residual is simplified
or explained away by this record. (Retrieve M07, M08, M16.)

| Difference/family | Printed finding and classification |
|---|---|
| SymPy face-step factor | As-computed differences occur at both faces in the balance/source distributions, traced distributions, face contributions/trace coefficients and material requirements, including `material/face_requirements`. They print `0` on the supplied domain. The build acceptance explicitly settles the representation: SymPy carries a step in `w₊−w₋`, set to one by strict face order; Wolfram does not. **Representational on that supplied domain**, with every raw difference retained. (M02, M07, M08.) |
| Six unrestricted coefficient diagnostics | `material/face/{1,2}/coefficient_operand`, `general_order/face/{1,2}/coefficient_operand`, `conditional_fast_ordering/face/{1,2}/coefficient_operand`: the only stored parts whose as-computed and supplied-domain residuals are not literal `"0"` are `regular/above` for face 1 and `regular/below` for face 2; their stored rational samples differ. Every other stored part, including the coefficient's own delta parts, has literal `"0"` in both residual fields. Under strict `w₋<w₊`, these differing regions are off the respective delta support. **Differences in labelled representation diagnostics, confined off delta support**, carrying no data about the S12 distribution there: the comparator contract item 3 says **"A delta's coefficient carries data about the distribution only on that delta's support"**, and labels an unrestricted coefficient a diagnostic, never the delta's part. Both raw expressions and every stored part remain printed/retrievable in M03/M07/M08/M17. No cause for their difference is inferred. (M14, M17.) |
| Domain Boolean representations | `supplied/domain`, `region_domains`, and both packages' `region/{1,2,3}/domain`: extra face-order/real-coordinate content appears in the as-computed XOR operands. Sources explicitly supply strict ordering and real coordinates (SymPy real symbols; Wolfram `Element`); the comparator prints `False` on that domain. **Representational under those supplied assumptions**, not a domain derived here. `conditional_fast_ordering/interior_range` and the supplied interior-range component already print `False` as computed. M08 retains the complete strings. **Representational wording**: the revision-4 face rule and accepted build checks explicitly settle their common function class, including source face parts. The acceptance says **"Its two parts are not counterparts, so its paired comparison carries no information, and no claim rests on it."** **Accepted pairing defect / identification metadata**, not a physics difference or evidence of equivalence. User: **"Accept, note the engine row"**, 2026-10-20. (M02, M08.) |

The exact literal census is **1514 `"0"` among 1574 as-computed residual fields**, **1556 `"0"`
and 12 `"False"` among 1574 supplied-domain residual fields**, and **98028 `"0"` among 99162
sample residual fields**; every other literal is retained in M08/M16. These counts include diagnostic
and Boolean rows and do not mean that every counted sample is an independent comparison. There are

Two acceptance qualifications travel with all claims: SymPy obtains regional objects by substituting
regional fields, so those objects are **not a test of its support encoding**; and dimensional
homogeneity is definitional for the inferred order-flux units. The acceptance reports reviewed
````````

````````text
be read as they close. The two 2026-08-07 prior-art entries retain their provenance; the second route is
recorded under Population passes.
O2 adds sources to six existing entries and one separate supplier/budget entry with no owner named;
its four adopted premises retain their
conditional-input labels in the O2 pass below. Entry counts for that pass are retrieved in
`steps/_measurements/O2_record_measurements.md`, M11. S9b adds one S21 requirement and sources
six existing entries; its before/after census is `steps/_measurements/S9b_record_measurements.md`, M10.
The light-closing pass adds one S22 entry and oracle-comparison evidence to `R-S21-01`; its lookups are
`directives/_measurements/light_sector_closeout_lookups.md`.
S12 merges its printed regional/face compatibility objects into `R-S12-02` and adds scoped source or
decision provenance to four existing entries; it adds no entry and closes none. Its retrievals and
before/after census are `steps/_measurements/S12_record_measurements.md`, M12/M15.
S14a part 1 merges projected/shell compatibility into `R-S12-02` and adds scoped
source/provenance to four existing entries, with no new entry or status change. Its census and
pass are retrieved in `steps/_measurements/S14a_1_record_measurements.md`, M12/M16.

## Why this exists

v3 is requirements-first: each sector states what it needs, and the knit happens last. The light sector
(S9, S10, S11, S11b) is built on **S1–S8, none of which have been run** — there are no step records for
them. That is the design, not an oversight: the substrate gets built once every sector has said what it
requires, so it can be checked against all of them at once rather than rebuilt per sector.

The bet only pays if the requirements are **captured**. Measured 2026-08-05: exactly two obligations are
recorded, both from S11, both about one quantity, both as inline anchors in a 1200-line plan. Everything
else a built sector assumes about the substrate is implicit prose spread across four step records.

`DEFECT_REGISTER.md` records what is *wrong*. `ANSATZ_LEDGER.md` records what is *postulated and not
derived*. Neither records **what a later step must deliver for an earlier result to stand.** That is this
file.

## The failure this prevents

At Phase 5 the sectors are knitted and the substrate is built against their combined requirements. If a
requirement was never written down, one of two things happens: the substrate is built without it and the
sector's result silently loses its footing, or it is rediscovered late and the substrate is rebuilt. Both
were survivable with one sector. With five they are not.

The S7 entry below is the shape of the problem. Standing at S7, the honest answer is "the slab width is
not selected" — a real result, bankable, and **insufficient**. Only S11 knows that the question must be
asked again with the width made position- and time-dependent. Nobody at S7 would think to ask.

## Entry schema

| field | meaning |
|---|---|
| `id` | stable identifier, cited from both ends |
| `source` | the step that needs it |
| `target` | the step that must deliver it |
| `requirement` | **the object required**, named — not a derivation path for it |
| `on failure` | what breaks in the source step if the target cannot deliver |
| `status` | OPEN · DELIVERED · RETIRED (with the commit that changed it) |

Name the object; do not prescribe how the target should obtain it. A requirement that specifies a
derivation route manufactures arguments about the route — see `CLAUDE.md` rule 3.

Distinguish carefully:
- a **requirement** is an obligation on a *future* step;
- a **postulate** is a value or form assumed *here* and not derived — that belongs in `ANSATZ_LEDGER.md`;
- a **defect** is something already wrong — that belongs in `DEFECT_REGISTER.md`.

A postulate with a named retirement condition generates a requirement. `B_comp` below is exactly that.
````````
