````````text
## PHASE 0 — the substrate (4 steps)

Re-banked from v2, forward, with the corrections this session established. ⭐ Cheap — but it is what
makes v3 readable without v2, which is acceptance criterion (3).

### S1 · The medium, and the brane as its ordered state
One GNLS condensate in a 4D bulk; `ρ = |ψ|²`; `P = Kρ⁵`; the brane is the **ordered state** of the same
medium (`χ_B = 1`), not a second object.
**Expected new:** 4 scalars `{ħ, m_GNLS, K, ρ0}` · 3 discrete `{D=4, n=5, the two-phase split}` · 3 fields
· 1 BC.
**Register:** none. **Carry forward:** the two-phase split is `postulated` — the largest tier-1 item.
⚠ **Correct v2's stated reason.** `model_map.md:26` justifies the postulate by *"`U(ρ)` is single-well"*.
That reasoning is **wrong** — the brane comes from `V_χ(r_B)`, a potential on a *different* field. The
conclusion survives, the argument does not. → S5.

### S1.5 · The substrate action and the Madelung balances ⭐ ADDED — was owed and unnumbered
The GNLS action, the quantum-gradient term, and the Madelung mass/momentum/energy balances, with the
pressure identity that fixes `U(ρ) = Kρ⁵/4`.
⛔ **Without this step the chain is broken**: S4 invokes a "core balance" and S12 needs the
momentum/energy partners, and neither exists in S1's output. *"Derive forward from what previous steps
banked"* is false at S4 until this is in.
⛔ `U(ρ)` is **not** a third field or an independent free function — it is fixed by the EOS.
**Expected new:** nothing beyond S1's primitives, if the action is faithfully transcribed. ⚠ If it turns
out to introduce something, that is a finding.

⛔⛔ **Order: S1 THEN S1.5.** This step consumes S1's primitives, so it cannot precede it. ⚠ Earlier
handoff text said `S0.5 → S1.5 → S1`; that was **wrong** — the correct order is
`S0.5 → S1 → S1.5 → S2`.

⛔ **`U(ρ)` is NOT uniquely fixed by the pressure identity.** `P = ρU′ − U = Kρ⁵` gives
`U = Kρ⁵/4 + Cρ` — the linear term is unconstrained. `C` is a **chemical-potential / phase-reference
choice**, and it matters to the energy and source ledger. ⇒ **State the reference that sets `C = 0`
explicitly**; do not present `Kρ⁵/4` as forced.

⚠ Also **fix the field count**: S1 lists three fields including `U(ρ)`, while this step says `U` is not a
field. Name the two actual fields and correct S1.
⇒ Bank an explicit, consistent `Π_Q`, `ε_Q`, `j_ε^Q` improvement convention — the three form one package.
⚠ This step supplies the **conservative left-hand sides only**; S12's non-variational source partners
stay open.

### S2 · The sound speed
`c_s² = (1/m)dP/dρ = nKρ^(n-1)/m`. **Expected new: nothing** — pure consequence.
**Finding to re-bank:** `[K] = M L^(4n−2) T⁻²`, so `n` and `[K]` are one structural choice.
**Register:** none. **Open:** O-02 (is `K`+`n` one entry or two — user call, still open).

### S3 · The dimensional foundation — three pins, not four
`{c_s0, ħ, m_GNLS}` over `{L,T,M}`: `det = 1`, rank 3, **nullity 0**. A complete unit system.
⭐ **This is the step that must state A1's lesson**, not just its fix: a fourth pin of dimension `L`
*must* produce `a·m·c_s0/ħ` — forced arithmetic. The **form** was never in doubt; the error was reading
it as a defining equation for a **defect** quantity.
**Register:** **A1** (retired — record why), and the ⭐ **kind-test** that A7's investigation produced:
> ⛔ Ask *"is this the same KIND of thing?"* — one number for the whole medium, or one per particle —
> **not** *"is this value right?"*. The latter passes the pin.

### S4 · Healing length and enthalpy scale
`h0 = m c_s0²/4`; `ξ_h = √(ħ²/(2 m h0)) = √2 ħ/(m c_s0)` — derived from core balance.
**Expected new: nothing.**
**Register:** **A7** — record both conventions and *why* the no-√2 form is forced by the BdG ratio
`(kξ/2)²`. ⛔ Do not "unify" them; they are different lengths with different jobs.

`W_slab` is `FREE-UNREDUCED`; *"double-well selects NO width"*. The kink gives one interface; the brane

Transverse and longitudinal sectors; the reduced `h`/`u_L` operator.

## PHASE 3 — the drain (3 steps) ⚠ where the interior is deferred

### S12 · The throat as a boundary condition
⛔⛔ **CORRECTED 2026-07-31 — this step originally wrote a drain law the user had already RULED OUT.**

The committed non-variational drain is the **dynamical `Γ_B` order-conversion** law
(`stage045_nonvariational_block_prep.md:23`):

```
∂_t(χ_B n) + ∇₄·(χ_B n u + J_χ) = n Γ_B ,    Γ_B = Γ_return − Γ_drain ,    [Γ_B] = T⁻¹
```

An **internal order-conversion at the throat** — the dynamical wall converting ordered↔disordered
material. Phase-conversion, **not suction**.

⛔ **RULED OUT** (`:19`, user, 2026-07-24): the G0-card `S_drain` **ρ-mass-sink + remote return**, i.e.
`∂_t ρ + ∇·(ρv) = S_drain + S_leakage`. It is **frozen-wall-premised**, and the standing principle is
*"the drain cannot be a frozen wall — several instances where we tried to freeze the wall and it screwed
up all the calculations."* ⇒ If a static limit is ever wanted it must be **derived** as the `ω→0` limit
of the dynamical law, ⛔ never posed as an independent frozen solve.

⚠ The G0 card's *structure* is still reusable — return controllers, `f`/`w` balances, `𝔅`/mouth/collar/IR
BCs, the `F_var+F_flux+F_𝔅+F_rad` partition — but it must be **re-hosted** on the dynamical law.

⭐ **Note how this error happened**: I had the "never freeze the wall" rule in memory and wrote the frozen
form anyway, because I transcribed the equation from a spec section rather than deriving it forward. That
is precisely what §0's side-by-side rule exists to prevent.
⛔ **All six source terms are `[OPEN]`.** The throat enters this ledger **only** as a boundary condition
of given strength — that is the whole content of the scope boundary.
**Register:** **C6**; **C1** (⚠ the drain law is *parameterized, not derived* — and `g_phys` was never
mapped; carry the caveat, not the top line).

### S16 · ⭐⭐ The worldtube reduction — the theorem the whole scope boundary rests on

⛔ **The load-bearing step of this entire ledger** — but ⛔ **not** because it "legitimises the scope".
Round 2: it does not establish interior-independence at all, so do not carry that framing forward. What
it gives is a **conditional response-side approximation**, and the honest use of it is to enumerate what
must be *supplied* for the far field to be computable without the interior.

⚠ **Read the actual statement, not the slogan** (`research/4d_2_5pn/paper/4d_2_5pn.tex:605-614`):

> *"For a **compact defect of size `a ≪ r`** in a smooth external field, the center-of-mass reduction
> gives the usual point-particle force law … `+ O(a²/r² · Φ_ext/r)` … so that the universal
> point-particle source **at leading order** is controlled by the worldline/worldtube multipoles rather
> than by arbitrary internal details of the defect."*

correction is `O(a²/r²)` — controlled by the throat radius `a`, which is **undetermined** (**C2**,
**D1**). So "gravity does not depend on the interior" is precise only to leading order; beyond it,
gravity depends on exactly the quantity this project cannot compute.

The Spin Problem's verdict is that *"you cannot get frame dragging from a compact 4D bubble; you need
the tail."* ⛔ **If the tail is real, S16's premise fails** — and with it the justification for scoping
gravity apart from the interior at all.

⛔⛔ **And it is weaker still — an external review found the theorem is on the wrong SIDE.** What the
corpus establishes is a **response-side** result: how a compact body *moves in* a given external field
(`M_A ẌA = −M_A∇Φ_ext`). It is **not** a source-side statement about what field a defect *produces*.
`4d_1pn_full.tex:657,:673,:695,:734,:825` additionally assume compact support, smooth external fields,
a vanishing dipole, a quasi-static conservative regime, **a calibrated potential `−Gm/r`**, and
**supplied mass and multipole moments**, with boundary flux discarded by assumption.

⇒ **Rewrite S16 as a conditional response-side monopole approximation** — ⛔ not a source-generation or
interior-independence theorem — and list `M`, `a`, the multipoles, compactness, source normalization and
boundary-flux suppression as **supplied inputs** that belong in S22's debt table.

⇒ **S16 must be run BEFORE S19 is trusted, and its result feeds back into the charter.** Two blocking
legs. ⚠ Likely outcome: **the scope boundary is materially narrower than the charter claims.** Say so

## 1. Order of attack, and the honest expectation

**S1–S4** are cheap re-banking; they exist to make v3 self-contained.
**S5–S8** are the honest core — three of the four bank a *postulate* or a *gap*.
**S9–S11, S15–S20** are where the earned results live.
**S0.5 and the substrate-action step are prerequisites** — ⛔ nothing is banked before them.
**S22 is the deliverable.**

⚠ **Expect roughly this shape:** ~6 steps introducing genuinely new inputs, ~8 pure-consequence steps,
~4 steps whose entire result is *"this is not determined"*, and 1 (S22) that is the point of the
exercise. ⛔ If S5–S8 start producing derivations instead of postulates, something is being smuggled —
that is the phase where the model is known to be weakest.

## 2. ⛔ Failure modes to watch, specific to this plan

1. **Re-deriving the PN ladder.** S17 is cite-only. It is audited and DOI'd; touching it is pure cost.
2. **Letting S22 become a census.** It is one table with loci. It is not a provenance system.
3. **Treating a clean close as validation.** See `CHARTER.md` §1.1. A tidy v3-gravity means the far field
   does not depend on the interior — already known.
4. **Back-filling `c_γ` into phase 0** to make the registry balance at S2. That is the exact error O-01
   recorded.
5. **Building registry machinery as a precondition** for banking a step. Add one quantity and one
   relation when a step needs them; nothing more.

### 6.1 Spatial source laws

Define the compact normalized polynomial bump

```text
B_n(q;σ) = Γ(n/2+3)/[2π^(n/2)σ^n] · (1−|q|²/σ²)² · 1_(|q|<σ),
D_i(y)   = B_3(x−X_i;a/4) B_1(w−s_i a/2;a/4),
∫d⁴yD_i = 1.
```

The global return kernel is

```text
R_0(y)=η_ret(|x−X_ret|)·½[B_1(w−2L;a)+B_1(w+2L;a)],
η_ret(r)=3·1_[R_ret,R_ret+a]/[4π((R_ret+a)³−R_ret³)],
∫d⁴yR_0=1.
```

`X_ret` is the fixed center of the outer return apparatus (the pair barycenter in a held two-body run), and the kernel is exactly `R_w` even.

The wall-to-drain map is the even frozen-profile projection

```text
N_d = ∫d⁴y D_+(y)[r_Σ,+²−r_bg²],
Q_d[r_Σ,s_i] = N_d⁻¹∫d⁴y D_i(y)[r_Σ,s_i²−r_bg²] = 1.
```

Let `Γ_i=Γ_0Q_d=Γ_0>0` be fixed throughput. The mass sources are

`S_drain=−Σ_iΓ_0D_i`, `S_leakage=(Σ_iΓ_0)R_0`.

They have dimension `L⁻⁴T⁻¹=(-4,-1,0)`, are `[POSTULATE]`, and have provenance “minimal smooth finite-size sink plus one two-sided global return.” Their integrals cancel exactly. `g_χdrain:=Γ_0` has dimension `T⁻¹`, star value `10⁻³`, tag `[POSTULATE]`; provenance: smallest explicitly nonzero weak-drain rate, coupled to the frozen sleeve amplitude through `Q_d` but independent of the electric-source normalization. Dynamical `δQ_d` feedback is zero in this fixed-`Σ` card, as recorded in §9.
### 7.1 Two-sided return/outer conditions

Use `Ω_IR=B³_{R_out}×[−R_out,R_out]`, with the two-sided fields related by the declared `R_w` action. At the outer boundary:

```text
ρ=ρ_0,       n·∇φ=0 plus the phase quotient,
r_B=r_bg(w), H=0 (static), u_L=0, h=0.
```

For a moving check, replace `H=0` by the outgoing condition for `O_⊥−(M₄/K₄)∂t²`; the uniform-translation frequency is `ω=k·V`, and the card's stationary solution is its subsonic `V→0` limit. The static order of limits is `R_out→∞`, then `R_ret→∞` with `R/R_ret→0` and fixed `Γ_0`. This entry is `[POSTULATE]`; boundary-data dimensions are `ρ:L⁻⁴`, `φ:1`, `r_B:1`, `H:L⁻¹`, `u_L:L`, `h:1`; provenance: minimal two-sided, zero-net-source return with no static absorbing layer.

For this check, every body-attached object is translated as `X_i(t)=X_i(0)+V_it`: `Σ_i`, `η_i`, `D_i`, and the fixed source `Q_χ=s_i`; the return apparatus remains in the medium-rest frame. The same six source functionals and coefficients are used. “Steady” values in this card mean the continuous `|V_i|<min(c_s,c_E)` outgoing solution followed by `V_i→0`, not an independently frozen snapshot. This is `[CONVENTION]`, velocity dimension `(1,-1,0)`; provenance: scope §5's V→0 guard.

Balance laws:

```
∂_t n + div_4(n u) = 0                                    (total, exact)
∂_t(chi_B n) + div_4(chi_B n u + J_chi) = n Gamma_B ,      [Gamma_B] = T^-1
Gamma_B = Gamma_return - Gamma_drain
```

`Gamma_B < 0` = brane→bulk de-structuring (drain); `Gamma_B > 0` = bulk→brane
re-ordering (return). Total material is conserved; brane-ORDER is not.
````````

````````text
## 5. Prior art — oracles only

Only this section uses web search. Access was checked on 2026-10-06. Comparison scope below is a proposed
use of an oracle, conditional on matching its assumptions; **none of its equations, results or material
models is a premise for O2**. Ordinary two-dimensional surfaces in a three-dimensional bulk do not by
themselves establish a three-dimensional brane law in a four-dimensional bulk.

The repository also names the inflow/river/acoustic-sink class
(`docs/medium_requirements_and_prior_art.md:246,263–266`, repository-root path;
`directives/prior_art_4d_bulk_search_prompt.md:33–34`). Its scope differs from the surface-mechanics
oracles: comparison of independently derived flow/background and mass-coupling content, rather than
selection of a brane stress law.

This is an oracle candidate for O2's stress/traction pairing and a declared elastic surface limit. Before an equation-level comparison, open the full text and check material-surface kinematics, dimension, exchange assumptions and linearisation. It supplies no brane constitutive selection. |

It is a possible oracle for a derived exchange-momentum law and its velocity-frame bookkeeping. No bending functional, modulus, equilibrium shape or holder is imported. It would check a restricted material limit, not the live drain mechanism. |

McCulloch (1994), “Stress-dependent finite growth in soft elastic tissues,” Journal of Biomechanics 27(4), 455–467, DOI 10.1016/0021-9290(94)90021-3.** **UNVERIFIED paper: only publisher bibliographic search metadata checked; opening the publisher page failed.** [Publisher citation](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/0021929094900213). | Candidate for checking an independently specified evolving-reference/growth limit. Biological growth is not identified with S12 order conversion or either paused reference limit. This entry supplies no material law or reference-transport premise. |

## Second route: what the analog leaves out, and who owns it here

The register's second route asks why a prior result was limited, and whether that objection is answered
here. The check and the quoted papers name the following objections. The check answers none of them.

1. **What drives the flow.** Visser's background obeys the force balance (6)–(7), which includes an
   externally imposed driving potential `Φ`. The check computes no force comparison
   (`force_accounting_Visser`). So it is not known whether this model's drain sustains the flow without an
   imposed potential. The drain's source and boundary data belong to S12 (R-S12-02), and the force balance
   to O2's carried handoffs.
2. **Where the material goes.** Visser's continuity has no source. In that setting the §7 velocity forces the
   `r^(−3/2)` density. Here the brane can exchange material with the bulk through `j_n`, which the oracles
   lack (`no_off_sheet_exchange`). So this model can carry the same flow in two ways: with that density and
   zero exchange, or with another density and nonzero exchange. The check selects neither. Which one the
   drain produces is again S12's object (R-S12-02), and whether the knit can meet it belongs to R-S21-01.
3. **Scalar sound versus transverse light.** The analog carries a scalar wave, while light here is transverse
   with two polarizations. A shared cone does not identify the two wave equations
   (`scalar_acoustics_vs_transverse_optics`). Even for scalar sound, Visser notes that the §7 metric is
   "conformal to the Painlevé–Gullstrand form of the Schwarzschild geometry but not identical to it". Its
   varying density therefore affects the "frequency-dependent greybody factors" (local copy, p. 15; C4). The polarization and direction exclusions stay as S9b scopes
   them; no owner is added.
4. **The kinematics of analog gravity are not its dynamics.** The survey's limit on analog gravity applies
   unchanged: a matching flow profile does not derive `GM` or a law of gravity. It stays where the S9b
   register pass lists it.

These questions are carried as handoffs, not as new requirement entries. The register's light-closing pass
adds this comparison to R-S21-01 as evidence.
````````

````````text
**First route / rest-on test.** The user chose **"Record as requirement"** after the two
step-list reviews. The object required is **the slow limit and far behaviour selected by
the slab's own dynamics**, for S14a's still-underived controlled return and profile-dependent
`J→Q` obligations. Its consumers are **S14, S15 and S20**: without that delivery they remain
**CONDITIONAL, not carried results**. This is a named future delivery condition for those
claims, not an assumed form or a defect in an accepted part-2 result. No such result exists.
The register method makes an assertion about a step with no record a requirement; this one
is explicitly required by the user, not entered merely because a future solve could use it.

The register's merge test is applied to the selection object and its joint deliverers,
not just S12's forms. No existing entry covers that same requirement with those deliverers.
`R-S12-02` covers S12's native source/flux forms, separate data and compatibility objects;
it does not cover selection owed jointly with S8/S22 and unowned face/interior laws.
`R-S22-01`'s joint energy-split obligation and `R-O2-01`'s unowned supplier/budget obligation
are different objects. The method keys each entry by its deliverer, so selection has its own
entry, **`R-S14a-01`**; `R-S12-02` is unchanged from `978c359c` (M12/M17).

The user's V2 choice **"All the slab's laws (Recommended)"** (2026-10-21) replaces item 4's S12-only target
sentence. Its source is `directives/_measurements/S14a_2_record_r0_review_disposition.md`,
"The user's decision on V2", committed at `12aa65d5` (M16). The selected orchestrator option says:

> "The selection is owed jointly by S12's source and flux forms, the slab's material law
> (S8/S22), and the face and interior laws, which have no owner yet. The register says so and
> states the missing owner as missing; it invents none. The record reports the disagreement
> with both sides quoted."

The new entry's target field and by-target row state those joint deliverers for selection. Existing S12
source/flux/boundary obligations remain with S12 under **"Keep S12"**. No missing owner is
invented, and the selection requirement does not repartition the complete return-law/map
obligations. Recording it delivers none of the forms, the slow return law or the source map.
The inherited real-fraction/A13, live-face and part-1
comparison qualifications remain. No new averaging operation, solution family, density gate,
limit order, drain/return law, material, profile, premise or candidate is prescribed.

The evidence is pass 1 Q1–Q3 and pass 2 R1–R3 as filed, not an accepted build or a universal
theorem: three pass-1 Codex instruments, one pass-1 Grok instrument, two pass-2 Codex
instruments and one pass-2 Grok instrument. Their constructions/checks differ, as record
§§2–3 states. In particular, pass-2 Grok's gate example checks one projected material balance,
not both native balances. The pass-2 orchestrator reading is quoted as a reading. The user
decision, not an extrapolation from these probes, supplies the requirement's standing.

All 18 existing entries retain their sources/targets/statuses. No new computed consumer of
their dimension, shear, stiffness, inertia, equilibrium, mode, width, power or energy-split
objects is supplied by this retrieval-only record. The repeated flow-framing decision keeps
S21's existing comparison obligation; no new S21 source/comparison result is added.

**Second route.** Apply the register's broader rule for every sector with identified prior
art. M14 retrieves O2 scoping §5 and the O2/S9b/light-closing/part-1 source inventories. For
each candidate individually—Gurtin–Murdoch, Abels–Garcke–Grün, Scriven, Helfrich,
Rodriguez–Hoger–McCulloch, Hamilton–Lisle, Visser, Unruh, Cahill and Sbitnev—applicability,
transferable historical objections/failures and whether those are answered here remain OPEN,
no owner named for a new part-2 check. All other individually named S9b candidates keep their
original OPEN handoffs and filed access/model qualifications. No candidate is applied or
selected, no literature verification is performed, and no part-2 prior result is reproduced.

The four inherited river/acoustic objections remain distinct: S12 source/boundary realization
versus O2's carried force balance; S12 produced density/exchange versus S21 knit compatibility;
the scoped scalar-sound/transverse-light exclusion; and analog kinematics versus dynamics.
None is answered here. MacCullagh's `R-S8-04/-05` stay OPEN. No new requirement rests on an
unapplied oracle; both-route questions are preserved, not treated as negative findings.

**Directive/register relation and disagreements.** The current contract defers to this schema,
rest-on test and both routes. Its original S12-only target sentence and the earlier option's
**"(the open S12 forms)"** gloss disagree with accepted S12's material/face/interior ownership
statements; both sides are quoted in record §6 (M01/M11/M16). The V2 user decision quoted above
supersedes that target sentence with joint delivery. The historical population
directive says **"Run it only on a prior result that a record in the reading list identifies
as reproduced by the sector"** (direct retrieval M11); this register says **"Run this route
for every sector with identified prior art"** (M12). The register governs. The pointwise-rate,
DC/slow-limit, historical material-conservation and S13-filing tensions remain quoted with
both sides in record §6 (M02/M03/M11/M13). The contract requests **"a committed generator script"**
and says **"No commit and no push"**; the list's own no-commit instruction governs this repair.
The round-1 reviewed generator and measurements are preserved at `85904c4e`; the repair is
uncommitted and claims no acceptance (M16/M17). No physics disagreement is closed by this pass.

# Project status — light sector closed for now; S12 and S14a part 1 accepted; S14a part 2 requirement recorded for review

On 2026-10-19 the user closed the light sector **for now**. Its accepted results are conditional. What stays
open is carried as requirements in the [substrate register](research/pde_ledger_v3/SUBSTRATE_REQUIREMENTS.md),
in its light-closing pass (accepted `e070c7f3`). S12's constructions (`a3da43d9`) and comparator
(`fe485b73`, with the engine-row note) are accepted. Its [step record](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S12_order_source.md)
and register pass are accepted at `ac909aaf` under "Accept, note both". Their known J1/J2 account/citation
items are repaired in the S14a part 1 record work.

S14a part 1's constructions (`94fddf2f`) and comparator (`de81b9fc`) are accepted. Its
[projection record](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S14a_1_projection.md) and register pass are accepted at
`4a4dfaf5` (round 1 clear), with [retrieval measurements](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/_measurements/S14a_1_record_measurements.md).
S13 is folded into S14a (user, 2026-10-21). This part prints the projected balances and spherical
two-channel `J`, signed order loss, storage, finite-radius flux and conditioned velocity objects.
It preserves the comparator differences and coverage limits. It chooses no flow or conversion law;
its historical handoff left the controlled return law with later S14a and the profile-dependent
`J→Q` completion OPEN.

S14a part 2's [record](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S14a_2_slow_limit.md) and register pass are written
for source-first review, with [retrieval measurements](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/_measurements/S14a_2_record_measurements.md).
The user chose **"Record as requirement"** (2026-10-21): no build ran. The required object is
the slow limit and far behaviour selected by the slab's own dynamics. The controlled
`ω→0` return law and profile-dependent `J→Q` map remain not derived. The filed witnesses retain
their separate pass/leg scopes as review instruments; they are not an accepted part 2 build.
The obligation has its own entry, `R-S14a-01`; `R-S12-02` retains its existing S12 scope.
The user's V2 choice **"All the slab's laws
(Recommended)"** makes selection owed jointly by S12's source/flux forms, S8/S22's material law
and the face/interior laws with no owner named ([decision](research/pde_ledger_v3/directives/_measurements/S14a_2_record_r0_review_disposition.md), `12aa65d5`). S14/S15/S20 remain
**CONDITIONAL, not carried results**, now on that named requirement. The two carried part-1
citation notes are repaired in its record; other part-1 claims retain their accepted standing.

**User decision, 2026-10-20: "Keep S12".** **"S12 stays open for the forms."** This record closes
only S12's requirements part and delivers none of the forms. The forms come once the brane is
defined, after the force sectors; every owner the sources and user gave is retained.

## Light sector at close

- **Accepted after S11c closed PARTIAL:**
  - O2, the steady brane balance: [record](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/O2_steady_brane_balance.md), `72866fcf`;
  - S9b, conditional light bending and radar slope: [record](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S9b_light_bending.md), `ff3bac63`;
  - the S9b flow-only oracle check, `8d91f448`. Its [record](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S9b_flow_oracle_comparison.md) and the light-sector closeout are accepted at `e070c7f3`.
- **What the oracle check shows:** S9b's flow-only squared speed equals the river model's and Visser §7's.
  This holds only if S9b's orbital GM, clock and speed units are identified with each oracle's, and the check
  supplies none of these identifications. The signed velocity then matches for one radial orientation. Where
  the velocity is nonzero, no exchange makes the brane density fall as r^(−3/2). The check does not show
  that the brane is that fluid, and its optics are a supplied input.
- **New requirement R-S22-01:** how the energy a throat releases splits among light, the extra longitudinal
  wave and the bulk. It is needed to decide whether matter observes S11's extra wave, and whether the model conflicts
  with the blackbody, CMB and N_eff measurements (photon survey E32–E34, E38). S22 owns the split and
  Q2 the mouth/held datum; S12 retains the throat's order-conversion source and its boundary data,
  as the user chose on 2026-10-19 and retained under "Keep S12" on 2026-10-20. Those forms remain OPEN.
- **Parked:**
  - S11c (PARTIAL);
  - every heavy backlog run ([DEFERRED_HEAVY_RUNS](research/pde_ledger_v3/DEFERRED_HEAVY_RUNS.md));
  - the applicability checks of the other named oracles.

## S11c, closed PARTIAL

The light model has a useful uniform result, but no accepted number for light lost at a defect.
S11c is closed **PARTIAL**. No nonuniform build, numerical continuation or review resumes automatically.
Read the [short closeout](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S11c_PARTIAL_CLOSEOUT.md) first; the
[canonical d record](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S11c_d_profile_conditioned_scattering.md) gives the evidence and limits.

## What later work may use

- **CONDITIONAL:** uniform light decouples from the bulk in the supplied linear model. SymPy and independent
  Wolfram work support it, with documented engine/repair reviews and comparison limits; stability needs
  nonnegative stiffness. [S11b, transverse mode and review provenance](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S11b_interface_coupling_law.md).
- **CONDITIONAL:** a later SymPy check found finite modes, nonzero energy current and zero face drives
  (light does not push on the bulk) at, above and below the tested light/sound speed matches, with the bulk
  at rest and other inputs fixed. Claude/Grok cleared the method, not a fresh review of the executed result.
  [Uniform completion, coverage/limits](research/pde_ledger_v3/_measurements/S11c_d_near_unity_uniform_continue_result.md).
- **UNRESOLVED:** the held-profile omega=3 benchmark at ratio approximately 0.12 found no numerically resolved
  loss. Its 1 ppm empirical floor is not a physical upper bound; it is not a matched-speed defect result.
  [Benchmark, signed-current table/controls](research/pde_ledger_v3/_measurements/S11c_d_numerical_radiating_balance_report.md).
- **CONDITIONAL:** four equation repairs have scoped support, not full composition clearance. Original b/c2
  checks were on pre-repair equations. [b changes](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S11c_b_variable_coefficient_operator.md#changed-after-close),
  [c2 changes](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S11c_c2_self_energy_fold.md#changed-after-close).
- **CONDITIONAL:** the bounded S10/S11 Lean contracts retain their reviewed proof status; they do not supply
  the missing physical scattering/work result. [S10 closure](research/pde_ledger_v3/lean/s10/FIDELITY_REVIEW.md),
  [S11 contracts and application limits](research/pde_ledger_v3/lean/s11/README.md).

## Next and paused work

**S12's record and register pass are accepted at `ac909aaf`:** its accepted constructions and comparator report the source
required by the committed order balance on live fields satisfying U1's support condition, including both material face
requirements and the labelled fast-ordering specialization. The six unrestricted face-coefficient
differences are labelled representation diagnostics confined off their respective delta supports,
not open differences in the S12 distribution; both raw expressions remain retrievable. The record
preserves all comparator coverage limits and the accepted engine-row
note. It supplies no drain law, throat solution or flow. Native source/controller forms, distinct
boundary data, native identifications and additional conversion partners retain their sourced S12
ownership and remain OPEN. None of their forms is delivered here; they come once the brane is
defined, after the force sectors.
The S12 pass left 18 OPEN entries,
with compatibility constraints merged into `R-S12-02` and the comparison of force sectors' flow
requirements owed by `R-S21-01`, consistently in its by-target row and requirement field.
[Record](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S12_order_source.md),
[measurements](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/_measurements/S12_record_measurements.md).
The S14a part 1 pass also leaves 18 OPEN entries, adding scoped source/provenance to `R-S1-01`,
`R-S1-02`, `R-S12-02` and `R-S21-01`, with no new entry or status change. Its printed compatibility
constraints merge into `R-S12-02`; S21 retains the comparison of force sectors' flow requirements.
The S14a part 2 pass brings 18 → 19 OPEN entries. Its new `R-S14a-01` selection obligation
has V2's joint deliverers; no existing entry gains a source or changes target/status.
No existing entry covers the same selection requirement with those deliverers, so it is
separate from `R-S12-02`'s S12 forms/data and compatibility objects. The record/pass
await source-first review; no derivation or profile is supplied by recording this obligation.
The light closeout was accepted at `e070c7f3`; its review no longer gates S12. The oracle comparison's
questions about flow drive separate S12's source/boundary realization from O2's carried
force-balance handoffs. Material destination separates S12's drain-produced density/exchange
choice from S21's knit compatibility (`R-S21-01`). Each force states the flow it
needs; S21 compares them, and a mismatch reopens S9b (user, 2026-10-19).

The [throat/EM notes](docs/light_em_investigation_handoff.md) remain **exploratory and paused**, as input to
S22/Q2 (with S12/Q3 connections), not adopted constitutive laws. Mixed numerical recovery, centre-drive
implementation and new leakage workers remain parked. [Closeout, paused notes](research/pde_ledger_v3/steps/S11c_PARTIAL_CLOSEOUT.md).

Cleanup is complete: history rebuilt and pushed at `bdc181c0`; archive tag `archive/pre-cleanup-2026-10-04` is on both remotes; nine uniform-cache runs are kept on local disk ([preservation list](research/pde_ledger_v3/cleanup_2026_10/FINDINGS.md#phase-5-preservation-list--keep-on-disk)).

+

+  **no owner named** for the face and interior laws, by the user's V2 decision at `12aa65d5`.
+- **status** OPEN
+- **requirement** — the slow limit and far behaviour selected by the slab's own dynamics,
+  for S14a's still-underived controlled return and profile-dependent `J→Q` obligations.
+  The selected steady-throughput and exchange-live decisions keep spreading, exchange and
+  storage live. No average, solution family, density gate, order of limits, law, material,
+  profile, premise or candidate is selected here.

+  at `12aa65d5` (M16). It replaces item 4's S12-only target sentence. The earlier

+  quoted in record §§4/6. No missing owner is invented. S12's source/flux forms and separate
+  boundary data remain OPEN under **"Keep S12"**; the complete return-law/map obligations
+  are not repartitioned. Recording this requirement delivers none of the forms, the slow
+  return law or the source map.
+- **evidence and limits** — The step-list pass-1 Q1–Q3 and pass-2 R1–R3 dispositions and the

+  real-fraction/A13, live-face and part-1 domain/comparison qualifications remain (M11–M13).
+- **entry rather than merge** — The register keys an entry by its deliverer and merges only

+### S14a part 2 pass — dynamically selected slow limit and far behaviour · 2026-10-21; written for source-first review
+
+Source: `steps/S14a_2_slow_limit.md`, §§1–7. Commands and literal stdout:
+`steps/_measurements/S14a_2_record_measurements.md`, generated by
+`scripts/S14a_2_record_measurements.py`. M01/M02 retrieve the contract and both step-list
+revisions; M03 the review dispositions, lookup existence and assembly-audit excerpt; M04–M10 each
+witness's complete source and saved stdout; M11 the accepted part-1/S12 sources and owners; M12 the schema/method/census;
+M13/M14 the plan/comparison inventories and second-route handoffs; M15 this pass and the
+exact ancillary diff against `978c359c`; M16 the record round-0 dispositions, V2 user decision
+and committed-generator provenance; M17 the record round-1 dispositions. No build or witness rerun is performed here.
+
+**Census:** 18 entries before (`978c359c`) → 19 after, all OPEN. **One new entry by target:**
+`R-S14a-01`, jointly S12 (source/flux forms), S8/S22 (material law), and **no owner named** for
+face/interior laws, as V2 assigns. **Existing entries gaining a source: none.** No existing
+entry changes target/status; `R-S12-02` retains its block and by-target row at `978c359c`.
+The part-1 pass changes only its acceptance heading/provenance to `4a4dfaf5`; its other
+content is unchanged.
+
+**First route / rest-on test.** The user chose **"Record as requirement"** after the two
+step-list reviews. The object required is **the slow limit and far behaviour selected by
+the slab's own dynamics**, for S14a's still-underived controlled return and profile-dependent
+`J→Q` obligations. Its consumers are **S14, S15 and S20**: without that delivery they remain
+**CONDITIONAL, not carried results**. This is a named future delivery condition for those
+claims, not an assumed form or a defect in an accepted part-2 result. No such result exists.
+The register method makes an assertion about a step with no record a requirement; this one
+is explicitly required by the user, not entered merely because a future solve could use it.
+
+The register's merge test is applied to the selection object and its joint deliverers,
+not just S12's forms. No existing entry covers that same requirement with those deliverers.
+`R-S12-02` covers S12's native source/flux forms, separate data and compatibility objects;
+it does not cover selection owed jointly with S8/S22 and unowned face/interior laws.
+`R-S22-01`'s joint energy-split obligation and `R-O2-01`'s unowned supplier/budget obligation
+are different objects. The method keys each entry by its deliverer, so selection has its own
+entry, **`R-S14a-01`**; `R-S12-02` is unchanged from `978c359c` (M12/M17).
+
+The user's V2 choice **"All the slab's laws (Recommended)"** (2026-10-21) replaces item 4's S12-only target
+sentence. Its source is `directives/_measurements/S14a_2_record_r0_review_disposition.md`,
+"The user's decision on V2", committed at `12aa65d5` (M16). The selected orchestrator option says:
+
+> "The selection is owed jointly by S12's source and flux forms, the slab's material law
+> (S8/S22), and the face and interior laws, which have no owner yet. The register says so and
+> states the missing owner as missing; it invents none. The record reports the disagreement
+> with both sides quoted."
+
+The new entry's target field and by-target row state those joint deliverers for selection. Existing S12
+source/flux/boundary obligations remain with S12 under **"Keep S12"**. No missing owner is
+invented, and the selection requirement does not repartition the complete return-law/map
+obligations. Recording it delivers none of the forms, the slow return law or the source map.
+The inherited real-fraction/A13, live-face and part-1
+comparison qualifications remain. No new averaging operation, solution family, density gate,
+limit order, drain/return law, material, profile, premise or candidate is prescribed.
+
+The evidence is pass 1 Q1–Q3 and pass 2 R1–R3 as filed, not an accepted build or a universal
+theorem: three pass-1 Codex instruments, one pass-1 Grok instrument, two pass-2 Codex
+instruments and one pass-2 Grok instrument. Their constructions/checks differ, as record
+§§2–3 states. In particular, pass-2 Grok's gate example checks one projected material balance,
+not both native balances. The pass-2 orchestrator reading is quoted as a reading. The user
+decision, not an extrapolation from these probes, supplies the requirement's standing.
+
+All 18 existing entries retain their sources/targets/statuses. No new computed consumer of
+their dimension, shear, stiffness, inertia, equilibrium, mode, width, power or energy-split
+objects is supplied by this retrieval-only record. The repeated flow-framing decision keeps
+S21's existing comparison obligation; no new S21 source/comparison result is added.
+
+**Second route.** Apply the register's broader rule for every sector with identified prior
+art. M14 retrieves O2 scoping §5 and the O2/S9b/light-closing/part-1 source inventories. For
+each candidate individually—Gurtin–Murdoch, Abels–Garcke–Grün, Scriven, Helfrich,
+Rodriguez–Hoger–McCulloch, Hamilton–Lisle, Visser, Unruh, Cahill and Sbitnev—applicability,
+transferable historical objections/failures and whether those are answered here remain OPEN,
+no owner named for a new part-2 check. All other individually named S9b candidates keep their
+original OPEN handoffs and filed access/model qualifications. No candidate is applied or
+selected, no literature verification is performed, and no part-2 prior result is reproduced.
+
+The four inherited river/acoustic objections remain distinct: S12 source/boundary realization
+versus O2's carried force balance; S12 produced density/exchange versus S21 knit compatibility;
+the scoped scalar-sound/transverse-light exclusion; and analog kinematics versus dynamics.
+None is answered here. MacCullagh's `R-S8-04/-05` stay OPEN. No new requirement rests on an
+unapplied oracle; both-route questions are preserved, not treated as negative findings.
+
+**Directive/register relation and disagreements.** The current contract defers to this schema,
+rest-on test and both routes. Its original S12-only target sentence and the earlier option's
+**"(the open S12 forms)"** gloss disagree with accepted S12's material/face/interior ownership
+statements; both sides are quoted in record §6 (M01/M11/M16). The V2 user decision quoted above
+supersedes that target sentence with joint delivery. The historical population
+directive says **"Run it only on a prior result that a record in the reading list identifies
+as reproduced by the sector"** (direct retrieval M11); this register says **"Run this route
+for every sector with identified prior art"** (M12). The register governs. The pointwise-rate,
+DC/slow-limit, historical material-conservation and S13-filing tensions remain quoted with
+both sides in record §6 (M02/M03/M11/M13). The contract requests **"a committed generator script"**
+and says **"No commit and no push"**; the list's own no-commit instruction governs this repair.
+The round-1 reviewed generator and measurements are preserved at `85904c4e`; the repair is
+uncommitted and claims no acceptance (M16/M17). No physics disagreement is closed by this pass.
+
````````

## M16 — Record round-0 dispositions, V2 user decision and committed-generator provenance

````````text

# S14a part 2 record review, round 0: dispositions (orchestrator)

The mechanical lookups behind each disposition are in `S14a_2_record_r0_review_lookups.md`, beside this file
(generator `_scratch/s14a/gen/s14a2_rec_r0_lookups.sh`). "P-n" cites a section of that file. Every computed result
quoted here is a leg's saved stdout.

## The round

- **Artifact.** Codex-written (session `01a160d3…`), uncommitted on `978c359c`:
  - the record `steps/S14a_2_slow_limit.md` (`cbd65642…`);
  - its measurements `steps/_measurements/S14a_2_record_measurements.md` (`d63a36d4…`) and generator
    `scripts/S14a_2_record_measurements.py` (`b9cf1509…`);
  - the edits to `SUBSTRATE_REQUIREMENTS.md`, `steps/S14a_1_projection.md` and `STATUS.md`.

  All six files are preserved unchanged in the commit that adds this file. All six matched the baseline after the
  round. The working tree was unchanged apart from the lookups file itself (P1).
- **Role.** A physics-bearing step record and register pass, reviewed until clear (record row).
- **Prompt and legs.** `_scratch/s14a2/rec/s14a2_record_review_prompt_r0.md`, sha256 `2e5b2b5a…` (P1). Both legs
  received it identically, in parallel:
  - a fresh Claude agent (Opus): `/var/projects/_review_scratch/s14a2-record-r0-claude/REVIEW.md`;
  - Grok (`grok-4.7`, effort high): `/var/projects/_review_scratch/s14a2-record-r0-grok/REVIEW.md`, exit 0.
- **Verdicts.** Claude: NEEDS REVISION, 3 findings. Grok: CLEAR (P2).
- **What both legs reproduced.**
  - Each reran the seven filed witnesses under `timeout 600`. Every stdout was byte-identical to the filed one.
  - Each regenerated the measurements file into its scratch. Both outputs were byte-identical to the filed file
    (P2).

## Findings

**V1. An instruction is attributed to the user again.** (Claude 1.) **CONFIRMED.**
- **The record and the register.**
  - Record §6: "The user's current instruction is **"Commit nothing."**", cited as "Retrieve M01; current user
    instruction" (l.245–248).
  - The register's S14a part 2 pass: "resolved by the user's current **"Commit nothing"** instruction" (l.1678)
    (P3).
- **The source.** The phrase is line 5 of the orchestrator's brief to the author. It is in neither the record's
  decision list, nor its lookups, nor M01. The list says "No commit and no push" (l.96) (P3).
- **The precedent.** This is the part 1 record's T3, confirmed and repaired: "The user did not say it" (P3).
- **Cause.** The orchestrator's brief repeated the sentence that caused T3.
- **What must be true.** No sentence attributes it to the user. The list's own "No commit and no push" is the
  source the record cites.

**V2. The deliverer of the selection is a source disagreement, and the record does not report it.** (Claude 2.)
**CONFIRMED.**
- **The two sides.**
  - The option text the user selected (orchestrator-written): "the slab's own dynamics **(the open S12 forms)**
    must pick the slow limit and the far behaviour". The list's item 4: "Its target follows the sources' owners (the
    S12 record for the forms, under "Keep S12")" (P4).
  - The accepted S12 record: "Interior-order law/history/strain; faces' law; source/flux forms | No separate owner
    named for the first two; source/flux forms remain S12's. Slab material completion remains separately S8/S22",
    and "Slab's material law; puncture interior | S8/S22 for the former" (P4).
- **The evidence the requirement rests on.** The filed witnesses vary the material velocity, the material density
  or the face position. In family A, `ω` enters only through `u_r`, with `gr = gd = 1/(r**2*h)`. In family B it
  enters through `n`. The pass-1 slow family keeps "the source densities … exactly the same" while `n_eps` and
  `ur_eps` vary (P4).
- **The record.** §5, §7, the register entry and `STATUS.md` name S12 as the target. They state S8/S22 and the
  unowned laws only as separate qualifications. §6, the section on source comparisons and disagreements, contains
  none of the owner terms: count 0 (P4). The schema's target is "the step that must deliver it", one step (P4).
- **Effect.** The register could hand S12 a requirement whose evidence varies fields whose laws the S12 record
  assigns elsewhere, or to no one. The disagreement is not reported.
- **Cause.** The orchestrator's option text glossed "the slab's own dynamics" as "the open S12 forms". List item 4
  then fixed S12 as the target.
- **Disposition.** Reporting the disagreement with both sides quoted is a repair. Who must deliver the selection is
  a concept decision, and it goes to the user before the repair is written.

**V3. The record and the register state different scopes for the required object.** (Claude 3.) **CONFIRMED.**
- **The record.** "the slow limit and far behaviour selected by the slab's own dynamics**, including the
  controlled return and profile-dependent source-map obligations" (l.186–188) (P5).
- **The register.**
  - "for the controlled `ω→0` return law and the profile-dependent `J→Q` completion" (l.653).
  - "This requirement delivers none of the forms, the slow return law or the source map" (l.666–668).
  - The pass: "for S14a's still-underived controlled return and profile-dependent `J→Q` obligations" (P5).
- **Effect.** "including" would make the target owe the return law and the source map, a new owner split. The
  record's own §7 says "No new split chosen".
- **What must be true.** The record and the register state the same object.

## Notes below the filter, handled with the repair

  - the `R-S12-02` rewording "The objects are" → "The printed S12 objects are".

- **Part 1 record l.366.** "The durable generator is supplied in the working tree, uncommitted". The generator is in

  6 property, whose census does not claim to be complete. **CONFIRMED, minor.**

## Notes, no change
````````

````````text
# S14a part 2 record review, round 1: dispositions (orchestrator)

The mechanical lookups behind each disposition are in `S14a_2_record_r1_review_lookups.md`, beside this file
(generator `_scratch/s14a/gen/s14a2_rec_r1_lookups.sh`). "Q-n" cites a section of that file. Every computed result
quoted here is a leg's saved stdout.

## The round

- **Artifact.** The round-0 repair by the same Codex author (session `01a160d3…`), uncommitted on `12aa65d5`: the
  record, its measurements and generator, and the edits to the register, the part 1 record and `STATUS.md` (six
  files). All six are preserved unchanged in the commit that adds this file, and all six matched the baseline after
  the round. The working tree was unchanged apart from the lookups file. The Claude leg's import of the generator
  wrote a git-ignored `scripts/__pycache__/` entry; nothing tracked changed (Q1).
- **Role.** A physics-bearing step record and register pass, reviewed until clear (record row). This is round 1, a
  full review: item 0 checks the round-0 repairs, then items 1–8 review the whole record again.
- **Prompt and legs.** `_scratch/s14a2/rec/s14a2_record_review_prompt_r1.md`, sha256 `12e9eb7c…` (Q1). Both legs
  received it identically, in parallel:
  - a fresh Claude agent (Opus): `/var/projects/_review_scratch/s14a2-record-r1-claude/REVIEW.md`;
  - Grok (`grok-4.7`, effort high): `/var/projects/_review_scratch/s14a2-record-r1-grok/REVIEW.md`, exit 0.
- **Verdicts.** Claude: NEEDS REVISION, 1 finding. Grok: CLEAR (Q2).
- **The round-0 repairs.** Both legs found V1, V3 and the two minor items repaired. They found V2's decision quoted
  with its source and the disagreement reported with both sides. Neither found anything else moved relative to
  `2ae682e4`.
- **What both legs reproduced.** All seven witness reruns were byte-identical to the filed stdout. The measurements
  file regenerated byte-identical, sha256 `feffe119…` (Q2).

## Findings

**W1. After V2, the merge into `R-S12-02` rests on the pre-V2 reason.** (Claude 1.) **CONFIRMED.**
- **The record and the register.**
  - Record: "The existing native drain/return/source/flux and separate-boundary-data entry covers the S12 forms, so
    this merges into `R-S12-02`" (l.211–212).
  - Register pass: "already covers the OPEN S12 forms, so the new scope merges into `R-S12-02`" (l.1648–1649) (Q3).
- **The sources.**
  - V2, as filed: the selection "is owed jointly by S12's source and flux forms, the slab's material law (S8/S22),
    and the face and interior laws, which have no owner yet". Item 4's "merge into an existing entry where one
    covers the same requirement" still holds (Q3).
  - The register's method: "Each becomes an entry keyed by the step that must deliver". Its schema: `target` is "the
    step that must deliver it" (Q3).
  - At `978c359c`, `R-S12-02`'s block contains none of "slow limit", "far behaviour", "S8/S22" or "no owner named",
    and its target is S12 alone (Q3). The record-list review recorded that `R-S12-02` "does not already state the
    new requirement" (Q3).
  - The register's precedents give an obligation with joint deliverers, or none, its own entry: `R-S22-01`
    ("S22 …, together with Q2 … and S12 …"; "The light-closing pass adds one S22 entry") and `R-O2-01` ("no owner
    named"; "one separate supplier/budget entry with no owner named") (Q3).
- **Now.** `R-S12-02`'s target field and by-target row split by obligation: "S12 for existing source/flux/boundary
  obligations; for S14a part 2 selection, jointly …". At `978c359c` and in the working tree, no other entry's target
  is split by obligation (Q3, the Claude leg's saved census of every target field).
- **Effect.** The census ("18 → 18", "New entries by target: none", "One existing entry gains a source") holds only if
  `R-S12-02` covers the jointly owed requirement. The stated reason covers one of the three deliverers.
- **What must be true.** The record and the register apply the register's merge test and its keying by deliverer
  to the object as V2 assigns it, and state the reason. If no existing entry covers the same requirement with the
  same deliverers, the requirement is an entry of its own. The census, the by-target row, the `STATUS.md` lines and
  the §8 account follow. No deliverer or owner is chosen beyond V2.

## Minor items, handled with the repair (Claude, below the filter; confirmed)

- **X1. §8 describes the repair, not the diff from `978c359c`.** List item 7 measures the account against
  `978c359c`.
  - The `STATUS.md` row says "V2 replaces the S12-only selection gloss/target". At `978c359c`, `STATUS.md` contains
    no "S12 forms". The gloss appears only at `2ae682e4` (Q4).
  - The heading at `978c359c` ends "S14a part 1 record written for review". Now it ends "S12 and S14a part 1

- **X2. A printed generator sentence is false.** M15 prints "New untracked deliverables do not appear in git diff",
  from generator l.175. The deliverables have been tracked since `2ae682e4`, and the `--name-only` output above it

- **X3. Five witnesses' "material jump" residuals cannot fail.** Their two operands are the same expression, or the
  residual is typed: `n*0-n*0`, `n*aminus-n*aminus`, `s.Tuple(0,0)`, `n*a_lo-n*a_lo`, `n*uw-n*uw` (Q4). The record
  says `p1-codex_check_face_total_scope` "checks material regular/jump residuals" (l.96, Q4). Item 2 describes a witness by what it constructs, checks and
  compares, and no more. A residual whose operands are identical, or one typed by hand, is not described as a check.
````````
